Compared with response-derived BMAs, which are more prone to trait drift, thought-derived BMAs more faithfully capture the intended behavioral mechanism and provide cleaner control over situated behavioral styles. Our results suggest that LLM personality-like tendencies are better understood not as abstract self-report traits, but as measurable and controllable behavioral modes grounded in concrete interaction contexts. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/lhz191/LLM-Behavioral-Personality.

</details>


### [144] [SQuaT: Self-Supervised Knowledge Distillation via Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10709)
*HyeonJun Lee,Hyeonsik Jo,Jinwoo Chung,Jangho Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SQuaT框架，通过在知识蒸馏过程中使用学生模型的量化参数来量化教师特征，从理论上消除了教师与学生模型之间范围不匹配导致的不可达残差，解决无标签场景下量化感知训练结合知识蒸馏的根本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中训练标签常因隐私、版权或成本不可得，知识蒸馏被用于解决此问题，但先前QAT+KD的工作存在根本缺陷：蒸馏时教师与量化学生模型之间的范围不匹配会产生不可达残差，导致蒸馏损失存在不可降低的下界，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出SQuaT（Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features）框架，一种无标签的QAT方法，核心思想是在蒸馏过程中应用学生模型的量化参数来量化教师的特征，从而理论上消除该不可达残差导致的蒸馏损失下界。

Result: 在多种设置下的综合实验表明，SQuaT持续优于强基线，尤其在极低比特（1位和2位）设置下增益显著；跨多种模型设计选择的广泛评估显示该方法不依赖特定架构假设，广泛适用于不同架构和量化设置。

Conclusion: SQuaT通过学生感知的教师特征量化策略，有效解决了无标签QAT中知识蒸馏的范围不匹配问题，具有广泛的适用性和显著的性能优势，特别是在极端低比特量化场景下。

Abstract: Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) enables the deployment of quantized models with minimal accuracy degradation. However, in practical scenarios, training labels are often unavailable due to privacy, copyright, or cost constraints. Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a common approach to address this challenge, but we observe that prior work combining QAT with KD suffers from a fundamental limitation: during distillation, the range mismatch between the teacher and the quantized student model induces an unattainable residual, resulting in an irreducible lower bound on the distillation loss. Motivated by this observation, we propose SQuaT (Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features), a label-free QAT framework with KD that theoretically eliminates this lower bound by applying the student's quantization parameters to quantize the teacher's features during distillation. Through comprehensive experiments across diverse settings, we demonstrate that SQuaT consistently outperforms strong baselines, with particularly pronounced gains in extreme low-bit (e.g., 1- and 2-bit) settings. Furthermore, extensive evaluations across various model design choices show that our approach does not rely on specific architectural assumptions, making it broadly applicable across diverse architectures and quantization settings. The source code is available at https://github.com/lcdbsa522/SQuaT.

</details>


### [145] [Long-Time Trajectory Approximation via SA-NODEs: Model Predictive and Floquet Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10738)
*Ziqian Li,Nikolaos M. Matzakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半自主神经常微分方程（SA-NODE）在长时间跨度下逼近动力学系统的问题。针对单一网络在全局训练时误差界随视界长度呈双指数恶化的问题，提出了两种基于状态重置的训练策略：模型预测策略（自适应划分视界，从观测数据重启每个窗口）和Floquet策略（针对具有稳定极限环的自主目标，在部署时无需数据即可将误差限制在线性增长）。文中还证明了时间周期架构下的轨道性保证及其障碍定理，并在四个基准上验证了误差定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经ODE在长时间逼近动力学系统时存在根本性的精度屏障：单一网络在完整时间视界上训练时，可用的误差界会随视界长度呈双指数恶化（double exponentially）。这一理论障碍严重限制了NODE在长时间动力学预测中的应用，亟需新的训练策略来规避这一壁垒。

Method: 提出两种基于状态重置的训练策略：(1) 模型预测策略（model predictive strategy）：自适应划分时间视界为窗口，每个窗口从观测数据重启训练，当每个窗口满足给定容差时，复合模型在时间上均匀满足该容差，且对于具有有界、均匀正则可达管的目标，参数预算随视界线性增长。(2) Floquet策略：针对具有稳定极限环的自主目标，利用学习得到的回报映射的认证收缩性，将误差限制在随周期数线性增长，部署时无需数据。此外对时间周期架构证明了轨道性保证（uniform-in-time orbital guarantee）及其假设以训练模型为测量对象，并证明了一个障碍定理：精确周期学习的场中，小单周期误差与收缩的频闪映射不能同时成立。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的数值实验验证了所预测的误差定律，并对每项保证的假设进行了测量。

Conclusion: 通过状态重置策略可以有效规避SA-NODE长期逼近中的双指数误差屏障，为长时间视界的神经ODE动力学建模提供了理论保证和实用训练方案。

Abstract: We study the approximation of dynamical systems by semi-autonomous neural ordinary differential equations (SA-NODEs) over long time horizons. For a single network trained on the whole horizon, the available error bound deteriorates double exponentially in the horizon length. We develop two training strategies that avoid this barrier, each built on a reset of the state. The model predictive strategy partitions the horizon adaptively and restarts every window from observed data: when training meets a prescribed tolerance on every window, the composite model meets it uniformly in time, with a parameter budget linear in the horizon for targets with a bounded, uniformly regular reachable tube. The Floquet strategy addresses autonomous targets with a stable limit cycle and uses no data at deployment: a certified contraction of the learned return map confines the error to linear growth in the number of elapsed periods. For the time-periodic architecture we deploy, the scalar certificate degenerates; we prove instead a uniform-in-time orbital guarantee whose hypotheses are measured on the trained model, and an obstruction showing that, for an exactly periodic learned field, small one-period error and a contracting stroboscopic map cannot hold at once. Numerical experiments on four benchmarks confirm the predicted error laws and measure the hypotheses of every guarantee.

</details>


### [146] [MoE Proxy Models for Low-Cost Failure Reproduction and Diagnosis in LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10823)
*Yikai Wang,Chuansai Zhou,Yuhang Zhou,Weiqiang Wu,Cong Wu,Yue Deng,Ben Feng,Mingming Zhu,Beirong Zhou,Zhibin Wang,Sheng Zhong,Chen Tian,Wangze Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了华为昇腾平台上大规模LLM强化学习后训练中的故障类型，识别了与故障复现相关的三个模型侧因素，并提出了一种基于结构保持和聚类专家剪枝的代理模型构建方法，以低成本复现和诊断训练故障，加速器需求降低50%-87.5%，单步NPU时长成本最高降低33.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的RL后训练计算密集且系统管线复杂，调试开销巨大。框架适配、数值精度、算子实现等因素会导致梯度溢出、损失发散等故障，而直接在大模型上复现这些故障需要大量时间和计算资源，缺乏低成本的故障复现与诊断手段。

Method: 首先对华为昇腾平台上大规模RL训练中的故障进行系统性分析，总结代表性故障类型并识别三个与故障复现相关的模型侧因素。基于这些因素，提出代理模型构建方法：采用结构保持和基于聚类的专家剪枝策略，选取代表性专家，同时保留模型的骨干架构、路由机制和基础任务能力，从而构建能反映原始模型训练动态的低成本代理模型。

Result: 代理模型将加速器需求降低50%-87.5%，单步NPU小时成本最高降低33.3倍，同时保留了主要的训练动态，并能复现与原始模型一致的故障响应。

Conclusion: 代理模型可作为RL后训练中故障复现、定向验证和辅助诊断的低成本替代方案，为大规模LLM训练调试提供高效支持。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) is computationally intensive and involves complex system pipelines with substantial debugging overhead. In practice, factors such as framework adaptation, numerical precision, and operator implementation can cause failures, including gradient overflow and loss divergence. Reproducing such failures directly on large models requires considerable time and computational resources. This paper systematically analyzes failures encountered during large-scale RL training on the Huawei Ascend platform, summarizes representative failure types, and identifies three model-side factors relevant to fault reproduction. Based on these factors, we propose a proxy-model construction method for low-cost fault investigation and auxiliary diagnosis. It employs structure-preserving, clustering-based expert pruning to select representative experts while retaining the model's backbone architecture, routing mechanism, and basic task capabilities. Our experimental results show that the proxy models reduce accelerator requirements by 50%-87.5% and achieve up to a 33.3x reduction in per-step NPU-hour cost, while preserving major training dynamics and reproducing fault responses consistent with the original models. Overall, the proxy models can serve as low-cost surrogates for fault reproduction, targeted validation, and auxiliary diagnosis in RL post-training.

</details>


### [147] [TACTICL: Task-Aware Compression of Tabular ICL Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10837)
*Mykhailo Koshil,Matthias Feurer,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACTICL是一种面向表格基础模型的自动化任务感知压缩框架，通过同时剪枝Transformer层并用轻量适配器替换来减少推理成本，在47个基准数据集上可替换高达85%的层而性能几乎不下降，且保持对数据分布的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格任务的基础模型虽然性能强大，但推理成本高昂。现有的蒸馏方法虽然能减少模型大小和计算需求，却牺牲了上下文自适应能力。因此需要一个既能压缩模型又能保留上下文学习能力的方案。

Method: TACTICL框架联合执行两个操作：1) 剪枝Transformer层；2) 将剪枝后的层替换为在下游任务上训练的轻量适配器，从而融合上下文学习（in-context learning）和权重内学习（in-weight learning）两种范式。

Result: 在47个基准数据集上，TACTICL能够替换高达85%的层而性能无明显下降，同时保持对数据漂移的鲁棒性，证明上下文学习能力未被破坏。

Conclusion: TACTICL提供了一个鲁棒的框架，通过结合任务特定适配和结构化压缩，有效利用了表格基础模型在深度维度上的冗余性，在降低推理成本的同时保持模型能力。

Abstract: The strong performance of foundation models for tabular tasks comes at substantial inference costs. Distilling models into task-specific architectures reduces model size and computational demands but also sacrifices in-context adaptability. Here we introduce TACTICL, an automated task-aware compression framework for tabular in-context learning models that jointly prunes transformer layers and replaces them with lightweight adapters trained on downstream tasks, thus blending in-context with in-weight learning. We study TACTICL on 47 benchmark datasets and show that we can substitute up to 85% of layers without substantial performance drop on a given downstream task. We further show that TACTICL maintains robustness to data shifts, leaving its in-context ability intact. Overall, TACTICL provides a robust framework for exploiting the depth-wise redundancy of tabular foundation models by combining task-specific adaptation and structured compression. We provide the code at: https://github.com/Hebog/tfm_compression

</details>


### [148] [Diffract: Spectral View of LLM Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10850)
*Nikita Borodin,Maria Krylova,Artem Zabolotnyi,Dmitry Aspisov,Egor Shikov,Nikita Tyuplyaev,Oleg Travkin,Roman Alferov,Dmitry Vinichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续预训练（CPT）如何将通用大语言模型适配到数学、指令、代码和自然文本等专业领域。通过SVD分析发现CPT主要改变奇异向量而非奇异值谱，注意力头存在强领域异质性，可移除多达60%的头更新而不损失质量，将低重要性头回退到预训练状态可使基准准确率提升至多4%，并发现领域间存在线性插值的平滑连通性。同时发布了Diffract开源工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练是适配大语言模型到专业领域的关键机制，但对其内部表征变化缺乏深入理解。理解CPT如何修改模型的权重结构，特别是哪些部分对领域适配真正重要，可以为更高效的适配策略提供理论基础。

Method: 对权重矩阵进行奇异值分解（SVD）对比CPT前后的谱变化与向量变化；分析注意力头投影矩阵的领域相关异质性；定义注意力头重要性准则；对低重要性头进行选择性回退（rewind）到预训练状态；在线性插值路径上评估领域连通性；构建Diffract开源工具包用于十亿级参数模型的谱分析。

Result: CPT保持奇异值谱基本不变，适配主要由奇异向量变化驱动；注意力头存在强领域依赖的异质性，可移除高达60%的头更新而无质量损失；选择性回退低重要性头使基准准确率比全量训练基线提升至多4%；线性插值CPT检查点可在不同领域间实现平滑质量插值；Diffract工具包支持十亿级参数模型的可扩展谱分析。

Conclusion: CPT对模型的修改是结构性的而非谱性的，注意力头的重要性高度领域特异，选择性回退策略不仅能减少不必要的更新还能提升性能，且不同领域CPT检查点之间存在连通性。这些发现为更高效、更可解释的持续预训练方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study continual pre-training (CPT) as a mechanism for adapting general-purpose large language models to specialized domains: mathematics, instruction, code, and natural text. Using singular value decomposition of weight matrices, we find that CPT leaves singular value spectra largely invariant, with adaptation driven mainly by changes in singular vectors. An analysis of attention-head projection matrices reveals strong, domain-dependent head heterogeneity, which we exploit to define a head importance criterion: up to 60% of head updates can be removed without measurable quality loss. Selectively rewinding low-importance heads to their pre-trained state improves benchmark accuracy by up to 4% versus the fully trained baseline. Finally, we identify domain connectivity - linear interpolation between CPT checkpoints yields smooth domain-quality interpolation without notable degradation on either domain - and release Diffract, an open-source toolkit for scalable spectral analysis of billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [149] [FiGuRO: Intrinsic Dimension Estimation for Multi-Modal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10857)
*Viktoria Schuster,Sana Tonekaboni,Caroline Uhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FiGuRO（保真度引导的秩优化）框架，用于在模型容量和超参数约束下近似单模态和多模态数据的固有维度（ID）。FiGuRO通过截断奇异值分解和自适应维度增减算法学习低秩投影的维度，并自然涌现出共享与私有信息的解耦，无需复杂的辅助损失函数。实验表明FiGuRO优于现有ID估计方法，对超参数变化更鲁棒，并可应用于现代预训练模型实现事后多模态表征解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定数据的固有维度（ID）对高效且可解释的表征学习至关重要，但在多模态设置中学习共享和私有信息的解耦表征时尤为困难。现有技术存在关键空白：它们通常是静态的、单模态的，或在对比学习情况下仅隐式适应共享ID。

Method: FiGuRO框架通过学习基于截断奇异值分解的低秩投影维度来实现ID近似，使用一个算法来决定何时在哪个潜在空间中减少或增加维度。共享与私有信息的解耦是该优化过程涌现的属性，无需额外的辅助损失函数。

Result: FiGuRO在ID估计上优于现有技术，对超参数变化更鲁棒。在模拟和真实数据上，FiGuRO能捕获不同的ID尺度和变化的子空间比率，成功分解共享和私有信息。此外，FiGuRO可应用于现代单模态预训练模型，实现高效的事后多模态表征解耦。

Conclusion: FiGuRO提供了一种统一的、鲁棒的ID估计与表征解耦方案，消除了对复杂辅助损失函数的依赖，并能无缝适配预训练模型进行事后解耦，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Determining the complexity, or Intrinsic Dimension (ID), of data is fundamental to efficient and interpretable representation learning. This is particularly challenging in multi-modal settings when trying to learn disentangled representations for shared and private information. Existing techniques leave a critical gap: they are often static, uni-modal, or in the case of contrastive methods, adapt only to the shared ID implicitly. We introduce Fidelity-Guided Rank Optimization (FiGuRO), a framework for approximating the ID of uni- and multi-modal data under constraints of model capacity and hyperparameters. FiGuRO learns the dimensions of low-rank projections using truncated singular value decomposition and an algorithm that determines when to reduce or increase dimension and in which latent space. Disentanglement of shared and private information arises as an emergent property of this optimization, eliminating the need for complex auxiliary loss functions. We demonstrate that FiGuRO outperforms existing ID estimation techniques and is more robust to hyperparameter changes. Across simulations and real-world data, FiGuRO captures distinct ID scales and varying subspace ratios, and decomposes shared and private information successfully. Furthermore, we show that FiGuRO can be applied to modern uni-modal pretrained models, enabling efficient, post-hoc disentanglement of multi-modal representations.

</details>


### [150] [Can Bayesian Optimization Efficiently Find a Strong Single Expert in Neural Thickets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10867)
*Nigel Bastian Cendra,Abdelhamid Ezzerg,Fernando Julio Cendra,Jeremias Knoblauch,Jakob Zeitler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯优化的无梯度后训练方法，在随机线性嵌入的权重子空间中进行高斯过程代理引导的候选评估，无需反向传播，以仅需RandOpt五分之一评估次数即可达到或超越其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无梯度后训练方法成本高昂，作者希望探索能否在有限的评估预算下，通过结构化搜索找到强力的单一专家模型。已有证据表明有用的权重更新位于低维子空间中，这为高效搜索提供了理论基础。

Method: 在权重空间的随机线性嵌入中应用贝叶斯优化，利用高斯过程代理模型来指导候选解的评估，完全不需要反向传播即可实现后训练。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，对Qwen2.5-Instruct（0.5B至3B参数）模型进行评估，贝叶斯优化仅使用RandOpt五分之一（即减少80%）的候选评估次数即可匹配或超越其性能。

Conclusion: 代理引导的结构化搜索能显著降低无梯度后训练的评估成本，同时产出更强的可部署单一专家模型。

Abstract: Gradient-free post-training has emerged as a compelling alternative to gradient-based optimization for large language models (LLMs), but existing approaches remain costly. We ask whether structured search can identify a strong single expert under a modest evaluation budget. Motivated by evidence that useful weight updates lie in low-dimensional subspaces, we apply Bayesian optimization within a random linear embedding of weight space. Our method requires no backpropagation and uses a Gaussian process surrogate to guide candidate evaluations efficiently. Across several reasoning benchmarks with Qwen2.5-Instruct models from 0.5B to 3B parameters, Bayesian optimization using five times less candidate evaluations matches or exceeds RandOpt. These results show that surrogate-guided search can substantially reduce the evaluation cost of gradient-free post-training while producing stronger deployable single experts.

</details>


### [151] [Benchmarking Time Series Generation Methods for Privacy-Preserving Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10891)
*Luis Amorim,Vitor Cerqueira,Moises Santos,Paulo J. Azevedo,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准测试，在"在合成数据上训练、在真实数据上测试"（TSTR）协议下，系统评估合成时间序列生成方法和基于噪声的匿名化基线方法，同时衡量预测性能和基于距离的经验隐私风险。作者还提出了Grasynda-P，一个结合矩阵集成和核密度估计的隐私驱动生成器扩展。结果显示：没有方法能完全替代原始训练数据；噪声匿名化隐私最强但预测最差；简单变换生成器优于深度生成模型；Grasynda-P位于Pareto前沿上，在竞争性预测性能与更强隐私分离之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私敏感领域（如医疗、金融）中的时间序列预测常常需要基于发布的数据而非原始观测进行模型训练。虽然合成时间序列生成方法已被开发用于数据增强，但这些方法在完全替代原始数据时表现如何、以及发布序列携带多大隐私风险，这些问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建一个系统基准测试，在7个数据集上统一评估多种合成生成方法和基于噪声的匿名化基线，采用TSTR协议联合评估预测性能和基于距离的经验隐私风险，刻画两目标之间的权衡。在此基础上提出Grasynda-P，对图基生成器Grasynda进行隐私驱动的扩展，引入矩阵集成（matrix ensembling）和核密度估计（Kernel Density Estimation）。

Result: (1) 没有任何生成方法能完全替代原始训练数据；(2) 基于噪声的匿名化方法隐私保护最强但预测性能最差；(3) 简单基于变换的生成器在预测任务上优于深度生成模型；(4) Grasynda-P位于Pareto前沿上，在保持有竞争力的预测性能的同时，比其他生成器具有更强的隐私分离性。

Conclusion: 该基准为评估和开发新的隐私感知合成时间序列生成方法建立了参考点，填补了合成生成方法在完全替代原始数据场景下预测性能和隐私风险联合评估的研究空白。

Abstract: Time series forecasting in privacy-sensitive domains often requires training models on released data rather than original observations. Synthetic time series generation has been developed primarily for data augmentation, where generated series supplement the original training set. How well these methods perform when fully replacing the original data - and how much privacy risk the released series carry - remains underexplored. We address this gap through a benchmark evaluating synthetic generation methods and noise-based anonymization baselines under a Train on Synthetic, Test on Real (TSTR) protocol. We jointly assess forecasting performance and distance-based empirical privacy risk across seven datasets, characterizing the trade-off between these objectives. We also introduce Grasynda-P, a privacy-motivated extension of the graph-based generator Grasynda, incorporating matrix ensembling and kernel density estimation. Our results show that: (1) no generation method fully substitutes for original training data; (2) noise-based anonymization yields the strongest privacy but the worst forecasting performance; (3) simple transformation-based generators outperform deep generative models for forecasting in this setting; and (4) Grasynda-P lies on the Pareto frontier, achieving competitive forecasting with stronger privacy separation than other generators. This benchmark establishes a reference point for evaluating and developing new privacy-aware synthetic time series generation methods.

</details>


### [152] [Partially Observable Learning for Multi-Platform Dispatch Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10897)
*Fengming Yao,Man Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出POLO框架，一种用于多平台即时配送系统调度优化的部分可观测多智能体强化学习方法，通过平台-网格独立代理建模、注意力机制策略表示和反事实奖励塑造，在部分可观测环境下实现优于现有方法的调度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中即时配送平台的骑手并非独占单一平台，可同时为多个平台服务，而各平台由于隐私和运营限制只能观测到自身的订单和骑手交互信息。然而现有调度优化研究大多假设骑手完全可观测且强制接单，在多平台部分可观测的真实场景下性能大幅退化。因此需要设计一种能适应部分可观测多平台环境的调度优化方法。

Method: POLO框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将每个平台-网格对建模为独立智能体，仅基于平台局部观测学习调度策略，符合真实隐私与运营约束；(2) 提出基于注意力机制的策略表示，选择性聚合骑手间信息以应对不完备异构骑手信息下的决策；(3) 设计反事实奖励塑造机制，缓解网格间联合行动导致的非平稳性问题，提升学习稳定性与可扩展性。此外开发了高保真模拟器评估多平台、多系统规模下的调度性能。

Result: 在多种平台数量和系统规模设置下的广泛实验中，POLO在平台收入和骑手行程效率方面持续优于强基线方法，验证了其在真实多平台环境下的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: POLO证明在部分可观测的多平台即时配送场景中，通过局部观测驱动的多智能体学习和注意力机制可以有效缓解信息不完备带来的调度性能退化，为真实多平台配送系统提供了可行的调度优化方案。

Abstract: Instant delivery platforms have become a critical component of urban logistics, increasingly relying on crowdsourced couriers to fulfill highly dynamic orders. In real-world systems, couriers are not exclusive to a single platform and may concurrently serve multiple platforms, while each platform can only observe its own orders and couriers' interactions due to privacy and operational constraints. This results in a multi-platform dispatch environment with inherent partial observability. However, most existing works on dispatch optimization assume full courier observability and mandatory assignment acceptance, causing substantial performance degradation when deployed in realistic multi-platform settings. In this paper, we propose POLO, a partially observable multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for dispatching optimization in multi-platform instant delivery systems. POLO firstly models each platform-grid pair as an independent agent that learns dispatch policies solely from platform-local observations, aligning the learning process with real-world privacy and operational constraints. To support effective decision-making under incomplete and heterogeneous courier information, POLO introduces a novel attention-based policy representation that selectively aggregates inter-courier information. Moreover, we design a counterfactual reward shaping mechanism to mitigate the non-stationarity induced by joint actions across grids, leading to more stable and scalable learning. We develop a high-fidelity simulator to evaluate dispatch performance under varying numbers of platforms and system scales. Extensive experiments demonstrate that POLO consistently outperforms strong baselines in terms of platform revenue and courier travel efficiency, highlighting its robustness and effectiveness in realistic multi-platform settings.

</details>


### [153] [ReOrder-OPD:Reliability-Aware Prompt Ordering for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10905)
*Ximo Zhu,Ruiqi Liu,Rong Wang,Ping Wu,Xiang Zheng,Wenzhuo Xu,Xubin Yao,Zhiyuan Yan,Bo Li,Jun Gao,Xiaolei Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReOrder-OPD方法，通过定义提示级别（prompt-level）的教师续写可靠度R来排序训练提示，以解决On-policy蒸馏（OPD）中教师监督不可靠的问题。R定义为教师从学生前缀到达正确答案的概率，由于直接估计R成本高，作者使用学生独立rollout与验证器正确教师轨迹之间的最大ROUGE-5 F1分数作为代理指标。实验表明按该代理分数降序训练优于随机和升序排序，并在Qwen3/Gemma4数学和代码任务上全面超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD方法使用局部置信度或师生一致度来加权、过滤或截断学生采样的轨迹，但这些信号无法直接判断教师能否从学生前缀续写到正确答案。此外，轨迹级别的干预会将单次rollout的不可靠性与提示的预期训练价值混为一谈。作者因此希望定义并利用提示级别的教师续写可靠度来更精准地指导训练。

Method: 首先从理论上定义提示级别教师续写可靠度R，即教师从学生当前策略诱导的前缀和轨迹中到达正确答案的平均概率。通过oracle实验验证R与OPD收益正相关。由于直接估计R需要大量教师续写，作者提出代理指标：计算一次独立学生rollout与验证器确认正确的同提示教师轨迹之间的最大ROUGE-5 F1分数。ReOrder-OPD按此代理分数对提示排序，然后对排序后的提示池进行独立on-policy轨迹采样，执行标准OPD训练。

Result: 代理分数等频分箱后，十个箱中平均R单调上升，表明代理能有效分离粗糙的可靠度层级。ReOrder-OPD在Qwen3和Gemma4数学设置以及Qwen3代码设置中，所有匹配的聚合比较均有提升。在全部六个FiRe-OPD和ExOPD设置中均获得增益，表明提示排序与轨迹内监督方法具有互补性。

Conclusion: 提示级别的可靠度排序是一个有效且正交于现有轨迹内监督手段的改进维度。ReOrder-OPD通过廉价代理分数实现提示排序，在不改变OPD核心机制的情况下显著提升了蒸馏效果，验证了"先选好提示，再做轨迹监督"这一分层思路的可行性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) applies token-level teacher supervision to student-generated trajectories, but this supervision is not always reliable. Existing methods use local confidence or teacher-student agreement to weight, filter, or truncate the sampled trajectory. These signals do not directly determine whether the teacher can continue a student prefix to a correct answer, and trajectory-level interventions can conflate one rollout's unreliability with low expected training value of its prompt. We define prompt-level teacher continuation reliability $R$ as the teacher's probability of reaching a correct answer from a student prefix, averaged over prefixes and trajectories induced by the current student. Oracle experiments show that high-$R$ prompts yield larger OPD gains and that descending-$R$ training outperforms random and ascending orders on a fixed prompt pool. Because estimating $R$ requires many teacher continuations, we use the maximum ROUGE-5 F1 between one independent student rollout and verifier-correct same-prompt teacher trajectories. Across ten equal-frequency bins of this actual score, mean $R$ rises monotonically, showing that the proxy separates coarse reliability levels. ReOrder-OPD sorts prompts by the proxy, then draws independent on-policy training trajectories for vanilla OPD. It improves every matched aggregate comparison across Qwen3 and Gemma4 mathematics settings and Qwen3 code settings. Gains in all six FiRe-OPD and ExOPD settings show that prompt ordering complements within-trajectory supervision.

</details>


### [154] [Physics-informed Diffusion Generative Model for Time-Series Data Synthesis in Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10941)
*Haiteng Wang,Yunfei Zhu,Tao Wang,Yikang Li,Jiabao Dong,Xiaoge Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhysDGM是一种逐步嵌入物理规律的扩散生成模型，用于合成与动力学系统物理规律一致的工业时间序列数据。该模型将物理定律嵌入生成过程的每一步反向扩散中，确保轨迹级物理一致性，而非仅在最终输出施加约束。利用PhysDGM构建的440万个样本（20倍扩展）的AI合成数据集在34个数据集上展现了强保真度，下游任务性能相比仅用真实数据分别提升48%（剩余寿命预测）、15%（健康指标估计）、22%（健康状态评估）和20%（故障诊断），且训练数据需求比现有方法少10-20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业时间序列信号（如航空发动机的涡轮温度和转速）对监测复杂动态系统健康状态至关重要，但受限于恶劣环境和高昂实验成本，此类数据的采集十分困难。现有方法无法在数据稀缺条件下生成与底层物理规律一致的高保真时间序列数据，因此需要一种能够在生成过程中融合物理约束的生成模型。

Method: PhysDGM采用逐步物理嵌入策略，在反向扩散的每一步中将物理规律直接嵌入生成过程，从而保证整个生成轨迹的物理一致性，而非仅在最终输出端施加物理约束。该方法构建了4.4百万样本（20倍扩展）的大规模AI合成数据集，覆盖涡扇发动机、航空发动机、电池和化工过程等34个数据集。

Result: 在34个数据集上验证了PhysDGM的高保真度。下游任务性能显著提升：剩余寿命预测提升48%、健康指标估计提升15%、健康状态评估提升22%、故障诊断提升20%。训练数据需求比现有方法减少10-20倍，并在航空发动机早期故障识别中展示了潜力。

Conclusion: PhysDGM为生成物理一致的工业时间序列提供了坚实基础，为将物理引导的AI推广到数据稀缺的多样化环境（包括工业机械和复杂化学反应动力学）开辟了道路，可大幅降低动态系统的数据采集成本。

Abstract: Industrial time-series signals, such as turbine temperature and rotational speed in aero-engines, are essential for monitoring the health and operational status of complex dynamical systems. However, collecting such data is often limited by harsh environments (e.g., high temperature and high pressure) and the high cost of experimental testing. To address this challenge, we introduce PhysDGM, a stepwise physics-embedded diffusion generative model for synthesizing time-series data that are consistent with the underlying physical laws of dynamical systems. PhysDGM embeds physical laws directly into each reverse diffusion step of the generative process, ensuring trajectory-level physical consistency, rather than enforcing constraints only at the final output. A large-scale AI-synthetic dataset (4.4 million samples, 20x scale-up) constructed by PhysDGM demonstrates strong fidelity across 34 datasets spanning turbofan engines, aero-engines, batteries, and chemical processes. After incorporating the synthetic data, the downstream task performance substantially surpassed that using real data alone by 48% for remaining useful life prediction, 15% for health indicator estimation, 22% for state-of-health assessment, and 20% for fault diagnosis. Moreover, it requires 10-20x less training data than existing approaches, substantially reducing the high cost of data collection in dynamical systems. We further demonstrate PhysDGM's potential in identifying early-stage faults in aero-engines by incorporating AI-synthesized data. In summary, PhysDGM provides a solid foundation for generating physically consistent industrial time-series, paving the way for expanding physics-guided AI into diverse data-scarce environments, including both industrial machinery and complex chemical reaction dynamics.

</details>


### [155] [GARLIC: Graph Attention-based Relational Learning of Multivariate Time Series in Intensive Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10969)
*Ruirui Wang,Yanke Li,Manuel Günther,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GARLIC模型，一种基于图注意力机制的神经网络架构，用于处理ICU不规则采样、高度缺失的多元时序数据。通过可学习的指数衰减编码器进行数据插补，利用时间滞后的摘要图捕获传感器间依赖，并结合跨维度序列注意力融合全局模式，在三项ICU基准测试中达到最新最优性能，同时提供可解释的注意力与图解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU医疗数据是不规则采样的异质多元时间序列，普遍存在缺失问题。临床场景不仅要求预测准确，还要求模型可解释。现有方法难以同时满足高精度与透明可解释性的需求，这是本研究要解决的核心矛盾。

Method: GARLIC由三部分组成：(1) 可学习的指数衰减编码器用于数据插补；(2) 时间滞后摘要图捕获传感器间的依赖关系；(3) 跨维度的序列注意力融合全局模式。所有注意力权重与图的边均端到端学习，内置观测级、信号级和边级三重解释。训练采用交替解耦优化方案，将辅助重建与主分类目标分别优化以稳定训练。评估包括特征移除（full > top50% > random50% > bottom50%的单调性能下降）验证注意力归因保真度。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、PhysioNet 2019和MIMIC-III三个ICU基准上达到最新最优（SOTA），AUROC和AUPRC显著优于最佳基线，且计算开销相当。消融实验验证各模块贡献；特征移除实验确认重要性归因的保真度；实时案例展示可操作的风险预警和透明解释。此外，在ICU之外的多种时间序列数据集上同样在插补与分类任务中展现优越性，证明其泛化能力。

Conclusion: GARLIC在不规则采样的ICU时序预测中同时实现了高精度与可解释性，解决了ICU数据异质性、缺失性和临床可解释性需求之间的张力，并展现出跨领域泛化能力，代表了不规则采样深度学习的显著进步。

Abstract: Healthcare data, such as Intensive Care Unit (ICU) records, comprise heterogeneous multivariate time series sampled at irregular intervals with pervasive missingness. However, clinical applications demand predictive models that are both accurate and interpretable. We present our Graph Attention-based Relational Learning for Intensive Care (GARLIC) model, a novel neural network architecture that imputes missing data through a learnable exponential-decay encoder, captures inter-sensor dependencies via time-lagged summary graphs, and fuses global patterns with cross-dimensional sequential attention. All attention weights and graph edges are learned end-to-end to serve as built-in observation-, signal-, and edge-level explanations. To reconcile auxiliary reconstruction and primary classification objectives, we developed an alternating decoupled optimization scheme that stabilizes training. On three ICU benchmarks (PhysioNet 2012 & 2019, MIMIC-III), GARLIC sets the new state of the art in outcome prediction, significantly improving AUROC and AUPRC over best-performing baselines at comparable computational cost. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of each module, and feature-removal trials validate the fidelity of importance attribution through a monotonic performance drop (full > top 50% > random 50% > bottom 50%). Real-time case studies demonstrate actionable risk warnings with transparent explanations, marking a significant advance toward accurate, explainable deep learning for irregularly sampled ICU time series data. Moreover, we demonstrated \proposed{}'s superiority in data imputation and classification on various time-series datasets beyond the ICU domain, showing its generalizability and applicability to broader tasks.

</details>


### [156] [DEFT: Data-Efficient Frequency-domain Top-k Sampling via Inverse Discrete Fourier Transform for Spatiotemporal Dynamical Systems Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11019)
*Hengbo Xiao,Jiale Liu,Jiahao Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEFT是一种频域数据采样方法，通过识别物理系统的主导傅里叶模式并系统性地变化对应振幅和相位来生成物理一致性的训练数据，同时提供了泛化界和选择K的理论准则，在多个PDE任务（包括电池退化系统）中显著减少了数据需求并保持高预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程（PDE）控制的时空动态系统建模面临两大挑战：物理仿真器计算成本高昂且需迭代数值求解；纯数据驱动模型依赖大量训练数据且对下游动态工况泛化能力差。

Method: DEFT方法：在频域中识别物理系统的主导傅里叶模式，系统地变化相应的振幅和相位，通过逆离散傅里叶变换（IDFT）生成物理一致性的训练数据。同时推导了该方法的泛化界，并提供了选择主导模式数量K的理论准则。

Result: 三组实验验证：（1）在典型PDE求解任务上优于传统方法（当系统由少数显著频率分量主导时）；（2）在PDEBench的扩散-吸附和Burgers方程上作为数据价值过滤器，数据需求减少40%，预测精度损失低于2%；（3）在电池退化PDE系统中，跨多个测试数据集R²均超过0.99，且学到的频域特征仅用20%的微调数据即可迁移到其他电池化学体系。

Conclusion: DEFT是一种高效算子学习的有效数据采样方法，能够以更少的数据实现高预测精度，且学到的频域特征具有良好的跨体系迁移能力。

Abstract: Modeling spatiotemporal dynamical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) poses two major challenges: it either requires expensive physics-based simulators that entail iterative numerical solving at high computational cost, or it depends on abundant training data, yet purely data-driven models often generalize poorly to downstream dynamic operating conditions. We propose DEFT, a frequency-domain data sampling method that identifies the dominant Fourier modes of a physical system and systematically varies the corresponding amplitudes and phases to generate physically consistent training data via the inverse discrete Fourier transform. In addition, we derive a generalization bound of this method. We note that it also provides a theoretically principled criterion for selecting $K$. We evaluate the proposed method through three sets of experiments, each targeting a distinct aspect of its utility. First, we validate the framework on canonical PDEs solving demonstrating that it outperforms traditional methods when the system is dominated by a few prominent frequency components. Second, we employ DEFT as a data-value filter on the diffusion--sorption and Burgers equations of PDEBench, showing that it reduces data requirements by $40\%$ while sacrificing less than $2\%$ in predictive accuracy. Third, to evaluate DEFT for more challenging and practically relevant problems, we validate it in the battery degradation PDE system, achieving consistently high predictive accuracy across various test datasets with $R^2$ values exceeding $0.99$. Moreover, the learned frequency-domain features transfer to other battery chemistries with only $20\%$ of the fine-tuning data. These results demonstrate that DEFT is an effective data-sampling method for efficient operator learning.

</details>


### [157] [Derivative Computation in PINNs: Automatic Differentiation, Finite Differences and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11020)
*Maciej J. Mikulski,Tadeusz Uhl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在物理信息神经网络（PINNs）中使用有限差分（FD）替代自动微分（AD）进行导数计算的可能性。研究表明，在三个基准PDE上，经过适当校准步长后，FD在精度上与AD相当，同时在全批次范围内运行更快、GPU内存消耗显著更低；提出的随机变体在平稳问题上优于AD。此外，对于具有样本间依赖的架构（如BatchNorm、自注意力），标准PyTorch autograd习惯用法是静默错误的，而FD提供了更接近真实逐样本导数的前向近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动微分（AD）是PINNs中导数计算的标准方法，但其在计算效率和内存占用上存在瓶颈，且对于具有样本间依赖的架构（如BatchNorm、自注意力）可能导致静默错误。因此需要探索更高效、更可靠的替代方案。

Method: 在三个基准PDE上系统比较FD与AD的导数值计算精度、运行时间和GPU内存占用；提出随机有限差分变体；针对样本间依赖架构，分析标准autograd的缺陷并提出FD作为前向近似方案。

Result: 经适当校准步长的FD在精度上与AD相当，在所有测试批次范围内速度更快且内存占用显著更低；随机变体在平稳问题上优于AD；在样本间依赖架构中FD比标准autograd更接近真实逐样本导数（高出一个数量级）。

Conclusion: FD可以作为PINNs中AD的有效替代方案，在精度不损失的前提下获得更好的效率与内存表现，并能规避某些架构下autograd的静默错误问题。

Abstract: We systematically investigate finite-difference (FD) derivative computation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) as an alternative to automatic differentiation (AD). On three benchmark PDEs we show that, with a properly calibrated step size, FD matches AD in accuracy on every problem while running faster across the full tested batch-size range and using substantially less GPU memory, and that a stochastic variant we propose outperforms AD on a stationary problem. We further show that for neural architectures with inter-sample dependencies (e.g. BatchNorm, self-attention) the standard PyTorch autograd idiom is silently incorrect; the correct per-sample alternative is computationally infeasible at PINN-relevant batch sizes, while FD provides a forward-only approximation that is empirically an order of magnitude closer to the true per-sample derivative.

</details>


### [158] [Mapping and Measuring the Behavioral Evolution of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11027)
*Dong Qiao,Chris Ding,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型响应行为的比较框架，通过三种句子级不相似性度量（对齐的逐提示平均距离、PCA压缩的分歧摘要、Gromov-Wasserstein几何差异）对6个模型家族的32个模型进行系统分析，发现模型家族形成连贯聚类、跨家族距离随时间递减、推理类模型响应云更紧凑，并建立了行为相似性与训练目标之间的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准排行榜只能衡量语言模型的性能水平，无法揭示模型间行为的关系或跨代际的变化。作者希望超越单一的性能指标，从行为层面系统刻画不同模型（及模型家族内部）对相同提示的响应相似性与差异性，并追踪模型随发布时间的演化趋势。

Method: 使用32个来自6个家族的模型对共享的10,000个提示词库进行响应；对每个响应进行嵌入后，构建三种互补的句子级不相似性度量：(1) 对齐的平均逐提示距离（响应上的伪度量）；(2) PCA压缩的逐提示分歧摘要；(3) 无对齐的Gromov-Wasserstein差异（比较模型内部响应几何）。通过这些构建，沿发布日期轴研究静态组织和时序变化，包括行为映射、家族漂移、层次聚类、跨家族趋同和响应云离散度。还基于逐提示最大均值差异（MMD）做了token级交叉验证，并建立了架构无关的充分条件，将行为相似性与推理提示覆盖率、小超额人口损失和相似的有效目标分布联系起来。

Result: 三种构建下模型家族均形成连贯聚类，gpt-2为全局离群点；跨家族距离随发布时间递减；多个近期推理导向模型具有相对紧凑的响应云。Token级MMD交叉验证与句子级平均距离高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.98）并重现相同定性结论。使用三种额外编码器（缩小至1/73规模）重新编码所有响应后，秩几何、离群点及时间趋势的符号均保持不变。

Conclusion: 该框架无需标签即可系统比较语言模型的输出行为，揭示了模型家族的结构性组织与跨代际收敛趋势。理论部分给出行为相似性的充分条件，可能为模型训练的统计特性提供解释视角。方法对编码器选择具有鲁棒性，具有较好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Benchmark leaderboards summarize how well a language model performs, but not how its behavior relates to that of other models or changes across generations. We characterize the output behavior of 32 models from six families using their responses to a shared bank of 10{,}000 prompts. After embedding each response, we construct three complementary sentence-level dissimilarities: an aligned mean per-prompt distance, which is a pseudometric on observed model responses; a PCA-compressed summary of prompt-wise disagreement; and an alignment-free Gromov--Wasserstein discrepancy between models' internal response geometries. We use these constructions to study static organization and temporal change on a release-date axis through behavioral maps, family-wise drift, hierarchical clustering, cross-family convergence, and response-cloud dispersion. Across the three constructions, model families form coherent clusters, with \texttt{gpt-2} as a global outlier; cross-family distances decrease over time; and several recent reasoning-oriented models have comparatively compact response clouds. A token-level cross-check based on per-prompt Maximum Mean Discrepancy closely agrees with the sentence-level mean distance (Spearman $ρ=0.98$) and recovers the same qualitative findings. We organize these comparisons through a measure-theoretic lens making their alignment and invariance assumptions explicit. We also establish an architecture-agnostic sufficient condition linking behavioral similarity to inference-prompt coverage, small excess population log-loss, and similar effective target distributions---a possible training-side account rather than an empirical explanation of the observed trends. Our pipeline is label-free, and re-encoding every response with three further encoders---down to one $73\times$ smaller---preserves the rank geometry, the outliers, and the sign of the time trend.

</details>


### [159] [ReRound: Reconstructive Rounding to Resolve Midpoint Ambiguity in Calibration-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11045)
*He-Yen Hsieh,H. T. Kung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReRound是一种后训练量化方法，通过条件扩散模型生成低bit权重的连续重建，用于消除标准RTN量化中中点附近的舍入歧义，在3-bit和4-bit量化下超越RTN及多种免校准方法，且全程离线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准round-to-nearest (RTN)量化方案在权重接近量化区间中点时存在舍入方向的歧义性，这种歧义会降低量化精度。现有方法难以在免校准的前提下解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 训练条件扩散模型从预训练LLM生成低bit权重的连续重建，作为消歧引导信号；2) 引入容差指标，度量量化权重到中点的距离，容差区域内的权重用扩散重建引导量化，接近边界的权重用RTN；3) 扫描容差参数生成多个候选量化矩阵，选择去量化后前导奇异值与原始全精度权重最匹配的候选。

Result: 在小规模LLM上，ReRound在3-bit和4-bit量化中一致优于标准RTN，超越广泛的免校准方法，逼近依赖校准的方法；完全离线运行，低bit推理阶段无额外开销。

Conclusion: ReRound代表了一种新的低bit量化策略，不仅适用于LLM，也可扩展到其他AI模型。本文重点关注其在小规模LLM上的应用。

Abstract: ReRound (Reconstructive Rounding) is a post-training quantization method that addresses the midpoint ambiguity inherent in standard round-to-nearest (RTN) schemes when quantizing weights near the centers of quantization intervals.
  Starting from a pretrained LLM, ReRound trains a conditional diffusion model to produce continuous reconstructions of low-bit weights for the LLM. These reconstructed weights act as a guidance signal to disambiguate the rounding direction of weights located close to interval midpoints.
  To integrate this reconstruction-guided rounding with conventional RTN, ReRound introduces a tolerance metric measuring how far the quantized weight (not the final quantized integer) is away from the midpoint: quantized weights within a tolerance region around midpoints are quantized using diffusion-based reconstructions, whereas weights closer to quantization boundaries are quantized with RTN. By sweeping the tolerance parameter, ReRound generates multiple candidate quantized integer weight matrices and selects the de-quantized weight matrix candidate whose leading singular values most closely match those of the original full-precision weights. This selected candidate determines the tolerance parameter ReRound uses.
  ReRound is particularly effective for smaller LLMs. Across a range of such models, it consistently outperforms standard RTN for 3-bit and 4-bit weight quantization. ReRound achieves superior accuracy compared to an extensive set of calibration-free methods, remains competitive with calibration-dependent approaches, and operates entirely offline, introducing no additional overhead during low-bit inference.
  The ReRound strategy represents a new approach for low-bit quantization. The method applies to AI models beyond LLMs. This paper focuses on its applications to small LLMs.

</details>


### [160] [Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning for Genomics Applications: Insights from an Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11054)
*Sepideh Saran,Mahsa Ghanbari,Uwe Ohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对深度学习模型在基因组学应用中的不确定性量化（UQ）方法进行了系统的实证分析，对比了Deep Ensembles、贝叶斯神经网络和MC-dropout三种方法，发现贝叶斯神经网络在类别不平衡和分布外数据场景下表现更优，并展示了不确定性分数可用于筛选高质量预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型已成为基因组学应用的标准计算工具，但该领域对不确定性量化（UQ）——特别是不同不确定性估计方法的可靠性——缺乏系统性研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，为基因组学中的UQ方法适用性和可靠性提供系统性评估框架。

Method: 通过一系列实验，在两类基因组学应用领域（序列到活性模型和单细胞表达分析）及不同模态中，系统对比了三种主流UQ方法：Deep Ensembles、贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）和Monte Carlo-dropout，评估它们在不同场景下（考虑常见数据集特征）量化不确定性的能力。

Result: 研究表明，尽管贝叶斯神经网络在计算上存在劣势，但它在捕捉由强类别不平衡和分布外数据引起的不确定性方面表现更好。此外，研究还展示了不确定性分数可应用于蛋白质-RNA相互作用中筛选高质量预测。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个系统的对比框架，为基因组学中UQ方法的适用性和可靠性提供了指导方针，明确指出了不同方法在不同场景下的优势与局限性，并验证了不确定性分数在实际生物信息学应用中的实用价值。

Abstract: Deep learning models have emerged as the standard computational tool for a wide range of applications in genomics. Yet, uncertainty quantification (UQ) -- and more specifically, the reliability of different uncertainty estimates in this domain -- has received little systematic attention. This work presents an empirical analysis of UQ in deep learning models, focusing on genomics applications. In a series of experiments, we contrast Deep Ensembles, Bayesian Neural Networks, and Monte Carlo-dropout methods. We assess their ability to quantify uncertainty in different scenarios, accounting for common dataset characteristics in two genomic application areas and modalities: sequence-to-activity models, and single-cell expression analysis. Our systematic comparison framework provides guidelines for the applicability and reliability of UQ methods in genomics, highlighting their strengths and limitations in different scenarios. We show that Bayesian Neural Networks are better at capturing uncertainty caused by strong class imbalance and out-of-distribution data in genomics, despite their computational disadvantages. Moreover, we show how uncertainty scores can be used to select high-quality predictions in protein-RNA interactions.

</details>


### [161] [Batch Size or Negatives? A Selection Rule for Memory-Constrained Recommender Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11061)
*Artyom Sabitov,Daniil Volkov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在大规模神经推荐系统中，softmax交叉熵目标函数的分类层内存开销巨大（O(nK)）。本文研究在固定内存预算B=nk下，采样softmax训练应如何分配批大小n和负样本数k。理论和实验均表明，最快收敛和最佳推荐质量源于n~B、k~1的配置，即应优先扩大批大小而非增加负样本数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样softmax通过在目标函数中仅包含k个候选负样本，将内存从O(nK)降至O(nk)。但在固定内存预算B=nk下，存在一个未被解答的问题：应优先增加批大小n还是增加负样本数k？这直接关系到训练效率和最终推荐质量，需要从理论和实证两方面给出指导。

Method: 作者在标准光滑性和方差假设下，对采样softmax训练进行理论收敛性分析，推导出在固定内存预算下的最优分配策略。理论分析表明n~B、k~1是最佳配置。随后通过受控合成实验和四个真实序列推荐基准（包括MovieLens-20M）进行实证验证。

Result: 理论分析证明在固定内存预算下，最快收敛来自n~B、k~1的分配。合成实验和四个真实推荐基准（含MovieLens-20M）均验证了这一结论：该配置比不平衡的分配方案收敛更快，最终推荐质量更高。

Conclusion: 在固定内存预算下训练推荐系统时，应尽可能扩大批大小而非增加负样本数量。这一发现为推荐系统训练中的内存配置提供了理论和实证基础。作者已公开代码和复现材料。

Abstract: Large-scale neural recommender systems are typically trained with a softmax cross-entropy objective over the full item vocabulary. For a typical large number of possible items $K$, the final classification layer dominates memory, requiring $O(nK)$ logits and gradients to materialize for a batch of $n$ examples. Sampled softmax reduces this cost by restricting the objective to only $k \ll K$ candidate negative items, resulting in an $O(nk)$ memory. However, for a fixed budget $B = n k$, it remains unclear whether one should prioritize larger batches or the inclusion of more negative items.
  We address this question by analyzing sampled-softmax training under a fixed memory constraint. Under standard smoothness and variance assumptions, our theoretical evidence suggests that the fastest convergence arises from an $ n \sim B, k \sim 1$ allocation. So, an actionable rule is to include as many objects as possible given computational constraints.
  Our theory is supported by controlled synthetic and synthetic and four real sequential recommendation benchmarks, including MovieLens-20M. The suggested configuration achieve faster convergence and better final recommendation quality than imbalanced alternatives within the same memory constraint. These findings provide a theoretical and empirical foundation for configuring memory during the training of recommender systems. Code, reproducibility materials, and all scripts for generating figures are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LimitedMemoryRule-BBFB

</details>


### [162] [Cross-View Feature Matching: Survey, Benchmarking, and Foundation-Model Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11093)
*Songlin Du,Xiaoyong Lu,Zeyu Wu,Xiaobo Lu,Guobao Xiao,Bin Fan,Jiayi Ma,Takeshi Ikenaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于跨视角特征匹配（cross-view feature matching）的综述论文，系统性回顾了该领域从任务特定模型向统一泛化对应模型演变的历程，提出了涵盖特征提取、单/多类型特征匹配器、视觉基础模型方法、训练策略和鲁棒估计的结构化分类体系，并在统一协议下对代表性方法进行了公平的基准测试，最后讨论了效率、极端条件鲁棒性和跨域泛化等开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管跨视角特征匹配领域在过去十年取得了显著进展，从任务特定模型发展到统一泛化模型，并受视觉基础模型推动，但现有研究在问题定义、模型架构、训练范式和评估协议上高度多样化，缺乏统一理解框架，亟需系统性的综述来梳理该领域。

Method: 本文提出了一种结构化的分类体系，涵盖五个关键维度：特征提取、单类型特征匹配器、多类型特征匹配器、基于视觉基础模型的方法、训练策略与鲁棒估计；在此基础上梳理关键设计原则，分析向统一泛化对应模型转变的趋势，并在一致的评估协议下对代表性SOTA方法进行统一的实验基准比较。

Result: 提供了统一的实验基准测试结果，对代表性最先进方法在一致协议下进行了公平且全面的性能比较；提炼了关键设计原则，揭示了该领域向统一和可泛化对应模型转变的明确趋势。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解跨视角特征匹配在视觉基础模型时代的演进、现状与未来发展提供了全面且结构化的参考，同时指出效率、极端条件鲁棒性和跨域泛化是该领域未来的重要研究方向。

Abstract: Cross-view feature matching aims to establish reliable correspondences across images with large viewpoint variations. Over the past decade, the field has evolved from task-specific models toward increasingly unified and generalizable correspondence models, with recent progress further driven by the emergence of vision foundation models (VFMs). Despite these advances, existing studies remain highly diverse in their problem formulations, model architectures, training paradigms, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to obtain a unified understanding of the field. In this survey, we present a unified review of cross-view feature matching. We first introduce a structured taxonomy covering feature extraction, single-type feature matcher, multi-type feature matcher, VFMs based methods, training strategy and robust estimation, providing a coherent framework for analysis and comparison. We further examine recent advances, distilling key design principles and highlighting the shift toward unified and generalizable correspondence models. We also provide a unified experimental benchmarking of representative state-of-the-art methods under consistent protocols, enabling fair and comprehensive performance comparisons. In addition, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including efficiency, robustness under extreme conditions, and cross-domain generalization. This survey aims to provide a comprehensive and structured reference for understanding the evolution, current landscape, and future development of cross-view feature matching in the era of vision foundation models.

</details>


### [163] [Two-stage Odd Residual Flows for Mean-Preserving Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11114)
*Kiran Madhusudhanan,Christian Klötergens,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TORF框架，通过两阶段方法解耦均值预测与不确定性估计，第一阶段使用预训练确定性模型获得精确均值，第二阶段利用严格奇函数的受限归一化流学习灵活残差分布，在保证均值保持的同时无需蒙特卡洛采样，在两个任务上实现了SOTA的确定性精度（NMAE）和密度估计性能（CRPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测方法在分布灵活性与均值预测准确性之间存在根本性权衡：参数化方法（如MVE）在联合NLL训练下会降低点预测精度，而现代生成模型（如归一化流、扩散模型）依赖昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样且可能产生次优均值估计。这种矛盾在长期概率预测中尤为突出，亟需一种能同时兼顾两者的方法。

Method: TORF（Two-stage Odd Residual Flows）采用两阶段解耦框架：第一阶段使用预训练的确定性模型生成准确的均值预测；第二阶段构建受限归一化流（Restricted Normalizing Flow），其变换函数严格为奇函数，用于学习围绕点预测的灵活残差分布。奇函数的数学性质保证了第一阶段均值在第二阶段被严格保持，且整个过程无需采样即可完成密度估计。

Result: 实验表明，TORF在短期和长期预测任务上均达到最先进的确定性精度（NMAE指标），同时在密度估计性能（CRPS指标）上展现出强有力的表现，实现了确定性预测与不确定性量化两方面的同步提升。

Conclusion: TORF通过解耦均值预测与不确定性估计的策略，成功解决了概率预测中分布灵活性与均值精度之间的权衡问题，为风险敏感决策下的长期概率预测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting plays an essential role in risk-sensitive decision-making, particularly in long-horizon settings. However, existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between distributional flexibility and accurate mean prediction. Traditional parametric methods, such as Mean Variance Estimation (MVE), can suffer from degraded point accuracy when trained under joint Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) objectives, while modern-flexible generative models, including Normalizing Flows and Diffusion Models, typically rely on costly Monte Carlo sampling and may yield suboptimal mean estimates. To address this limitation, we propose Two-stage Odd Residual Flows (TORF), a framework that decouples mean forecasting from uncertainty estimation. In the first stage, a pre-trained deterministic model is used to produce an accurate mean prediction. In the second stage, a Restricted Normalizing Flow, with strictly odd functions learns flexible residual distributions around the point forecast, guaranteeing mean preservation from the first stage without sampling. Experiments show that TORF achieves state-of-the-art deterministic accuracy (NMAE) while providing strong density estimation performance (CRPS) on short and long-horizon forecasting.

</details>


### [164] [A Recommendation System Approach for Interference-Robust Sensor Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11143)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Kyle Pak,Merve Karakas,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于推荐系统思路的传感器子集选择方法，利用频带声学特征和双塔多层感知机（Two-Tower MLP）架构替代传统RSSI方案，在户外车辆跟踪部署中将跟踪精度提升约20%，同时保持适用于实时选择性感知的低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于RSSI的传感器子集选择方法虽然高效，但容易受到声学干扰的影响，导致推荐的传感器子集跟踪精度下降。需要一种更能抵抗声学干扰且仍然保持低计算开销的方法。

Method: 提出一种受推荐系统启发的框架：利用频带声学特征（而非RSSI）作为输入，采用双塔多层感知机（Two-Tower MLP）架构对候选传感器子集进行高效评分，从而选出最优传感器子集。

Result: 在户外车辆跟踪实际部署中，相比RSSI基线方法，跟踪精度提升约20%，同时保持了实时选择性传感所需的低计算开销。

Conclusion: 频带声学特征结合双塔MLP架构能够有效提升传感器子集选择的鲁棒性和跟踪精度，在对抗声学干扰的同时兼顾了实时性要求，为低成本声学信号驱动的选择性感知提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: This paper develops a method for sensor-subset selection for tracking. Prior work showed that low-cost acoustic Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) measurements can be used to recommend subsets of sensor nodes whose expensive sensing modalities, such as cameras, can achieve high tracking accuracy. While efficient, RSSI-based approaches are challenged by acoustic interference. We propose a recommendation-system-inspired framework that instead leverages frequency-band acoustic features and a Two-Tower Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) architecture to efficiently score candidate sensor subsets. Experimental results on outdoor vehicle-tracking deployments show that the proposed method can improve accuracy by around 20\% over the RSSI baseline while maintaining the low computational overhead required for real-time selective sensing.

</details>


### [165] [The Gaussian-Multinoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine: A Potts Model Extension of the GRBM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11635)
*Nikhil Kapasi,Mohamed Elfouly,William Whitehead,Luke Theogarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯-多项逻辑斯谛受限玻尔兹曼机（GM-RBM），用q态分类（Potts）隐藏单元替代高斯-伯努利RBM中的二值隐藏单元，为多值概念提供更丰富的潜在状态空间。作者给出了能量函数、条件分布和学习规则的自包含推导，并介绍了避免状态坍缩的训练技巧（对比散度+温度退火+槽内多样性约束）。在容量匹配和参数匹配两种设置下与GB-RBM对比，GM-RBM在类比回忆和结构化记忆基准上达到同等或更优的召回率，且训练成本相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实任务（如联想记忆、符号推理）受益于离散、结构化的表征，而标准的连续潜变量模型难以表达这类表征。现有的高斯-伯努利RBM仅使用二值隐藏单元，表达能力受限，需要一种支持多值范畴概念的生成式能量模型。

Method: 将GB-RBM中的二值隐藏单元替换为q态分类（Potts）单元，构建GM-RBM模型；自包含推导能量函数、条件分布与学习规则；采用对比散度结合温度退火及槽内多样性约束的训练策略以规避状态坍缩；在容量匹配与参数匹配两种对照设置下与GB-RBM进行实验比较。

Result: 在类比回忆与结构化记忆基准上，GM-RBM在同等容量下取得与GB-RBM竞争甚至更优的召回率，训练成本相当，且仅使用Gibbs更新即可实现。离散q元公式化还便于高效实现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于可处理的RBM框架内的离散推理任务，分类隐藏单元可以作为二值隐藏单元的一种简单、可扩展的替代方案，尤其在需要多值概念表达的场景中具有优势。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks, from associative memory to symbolic reasoning, benefit from discrete, structured representations that standard continuous latent models can struggle to express. We introduce the Gaussian-Multinoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine (GM-RBM), a generative energy-based model that extends the Gaussian-Bernoulli RBM (GB-RBM) by replacing binary hidden units with q-state categorical (Potts) units, yielding a richer latent state space for multivalued concepts. We provide a self-contained derivation of the energy, conditional distributions, and learning rules, and detail practical training choices (contrastive divergence with temperature annealing and intra-slot diversity constraints) that avoid state collapse. To separate architectural effects from sheer latent capacity, we evaluate under both capacity-matched and parameter-matched setups, comparing GM-RBM with GB-RBM configured to have the same number of possible latent assignments. On analogical recall and structured memory benchmarks, GM-RBM achieves competitive, and in several regimes improved, recall at equal capacity with comparable training cost, despite using only Gibbs updates. The discrete q-ary formulation is also amenable to efficient implementation. These results clarify when categorical hidden units provide a simple, scalable alternative to binary latents for discrete inference within tractable RBMs.

</details>


### [166] [DACRI: Decision-Aware Causal Intervention Ranking for Critical Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11154)
*Shiqi Huang,Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Jize Li,Yan Lyu,Lashimi Muraleedharan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CriticalSCM-Bench v1，一个具有因果地面真值、配对事实/反事实推演和显式净价值目标的受控合成基准。实验表明LambdaMART在半导体和关键材料场景提升中位净价值5.7–16.2%，但在数字基础设施上简单的常量缓冲策略更优；部分和延迟观测下LambdaMART能保留33–75%的完全信息价值，并识别出适应性排序与简单结构化策略各自适用的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测或归因供应链中断并不等同于选择能最大化可恢复净价值的干预措施。现有研究缺乏一个将因果推理与干预选择（决策优化）相区分的受控基准，难以系统评估不同策略在供应链恢复中的价值表现。

Method: 构建CriticalSCM-Bench v1受控合成基准，包含因果地面真值、配对事实/反事实轨迹推演和显式净价值目标函数。对比LambdaMART自适应排序模型与全信息静态基准、领域信息常量缓冲策略，在部分/延迟观测、干预保真度/时机/成本扰动及留出中断场景下进行压力测试；另设计430次生成守卫式解释研究，进行确定性验证和模板回退。

Result: LambdaMART相对全信息基准中位提升5.7–16.2%，在半导体和关键材料场景有配对统计支持但数字基础设施场景无；数字基础设施场景中常量缓冲策略更强。部分和延迟设置下保留33–75%全信息价值。压力测试显示干预保真度、时机、成本及留出中断均能改变策略排序，关键材料场景的分布外保留最弱。守卫式解释中所有固定干预决策在确定性验证和模板回退后得到保留但措辞不稳定。

Conclusion: 模型复杂度并非总被证明合理：存在适应性排序能增加价值与简单结构化策略更优的两种不同"机制区间"。研究为在该受控环境中何时应选用自适应与非自适应干预策略提供了判别证据。

Abstract: Detecting or attributing a supply-chain disruption is not the same as selecting the intervention that maximizes recoverable net value. We present CriticalSCM-Bench v1, a controlled synthetic benchmark with causal ground truth, paired factual/counterfactual rollouts, and an explicit net-value objective. Relative to a full-information train-selected static benchmark, LambdaMART improves median normalized net value by 5.7--16.2\%, with paired statistical support on the semiconductor and critical-material archetypes but not on digital infrastructure. On digital infrastructure, a domain-informed constant-buffer policy remains stronger, showing that greater model complexity is not uniformly justified. Across partial and delayed settings, LambdaMART retains 33--75\% of full-clamp value. Stress tests further show that intervention fidelity, timing, cost, and held-out disruptions can alter policy ordering. Critical materials show the weakest out-of-distribution retention. Separately, a guarded explanation study over 540 generations preserves every fixed intervention decision after deterministic validation and template fallback, although exact wording remains unstable. Within this controlled setting, the results identify regimes in which adaptive ranking adds value and those in which simpler structural policies remain preferable.

</details>


### [167] [Hierarchical Empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes: Minimax Smoothing and Calibration with AODE Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11162)
*Nguyen Thai Anh,Truong Viet Vu,Tran Thien Thanh,Vo Nguyen Quoc Bao,Ngo Hoang Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层次经验贝叶斯朴素贝叶斯（HEB-NB）方法，通过Type-II极大似然自适应学习Dirichlet先验浓度，替代传统固定平滑强度的平滑规则（如Laplace等），并扩展到AODE结构松弛版本。理论证明其ℓ1误差界达到经验分布极小极大速率加可消失偏差，且与Laplace存在严格分离；实验表明在31个基准数据集上取得最优平均Friedman排名， log-loss最高降低22.1%，并显著改善校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的朴素贝叶斯平滑规则（Laplace、Lidstone、Krichevsky-Trofimov和m估计）都使用固定的平滑强度，忽略了特征基数、样本量和类别不平衡等因素，这在高基数表格数据上引入了不可消除的偏差。因此需要一种能够自适应数据特性、在不同类别间进行信息共享的平滑方法。

Method: 提出HEB-NB：对每个类别-特征的条件概率使用Dirichlet先验进行平滑，其中浓度参数通过Type-II极大似然（经验贝叶斯）从数据中自适应学习，实现跨类别的原理性信息共享并保持闭式推断。进一步将自适应平滑推广到AODE（平均单依赖估计）结构松弛，得到HEB-AODE。理论分析包括非渐近ℓ1误差界、Laplace紧的下界、plug-in超额贝叶斯风险界（通过总变差张量化）以及种群top-1期望校准误差（ECE）推论。

Result: 在31个UCI和OpenML基准数据集上，HEB-NB在概率指标上取得最佳平均Friedman排名，在高基数数据集上log-loss最多降低22.1%；HEB-AODE相比原始AODE保持一致改进；结合互信息加权后top-1 ECE降低41%-70%，在概率准确性和校准方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 自适应数据驱动的平滑方法（如HEB）相比固定平滑强度的传统规则，在高基数分类数据上具有理论与实践双重优势。该方法不仅提升了朴素贝叶斯的概率预测精度，还改善了模型校准性能，并且可以自然迁移到更复杂的贝叶斯网络结构松弛方法中。

Abstract: The Naive Bayes (NB) classifier remains a standard choice for categorical data, yet its widely used smoothing rules, such as Laplace, Lidstone, Krichevsky-Trofimov, and the $m$-estimate, all prescribe a fixed smoothing strength that ignores feature cardinality, sample size, and class imbalance, inducing a non-vanishing bias on modern high-cardinality tabular data. We propose hierarchical empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes (HEB-NB), in which each class-feature conditional probability is smoothed by a Dirichlet prior whose concentration is learned data-adaptively via Type-II maximum likelihood, enabling principled information sharing across classes while retaining closed-form inference. We further introduce HEB average one-dependence estimators (HEB-AODE), showing that the adaptive smoothing transfers cleanly to structural relaxations of NB. Theoretically, we establish a non-asymptotic $\ell_1$ error bound for HEB-NB matching the empirical-distribution minimax rate plus a vanishing data-adaptive bias, together with a matching Laplace-tight lower bound that yields a finite-sample, risk-level strict separation from Laplace. We further derive a plug-in excess Bayes-risk bound via total-variation tensorization and a population top-1 expected calibration error (ECE) corollary. Empirically, across 31 UCI and OpenML benchmarks, HEB-NB attains the best average Friedman rank on probabilistic metrics, with up to 22.1% log-loss reductions on high-cardinality datasets and consistent improvements of HEB-AODE over vanilla AODE. Combining HEB-NB with mutual-information weighting reduces top-1 ECE by 41%-70%, demonstrating substantial gains in probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond a Bag of Features: Set-Level Instability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11197)
*Nikolai Bolik,Lennart Stöpler,Artur Andrzejak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Shani等人（2026）关于LLM表征能恢复人类概念边界但无法反映细粒度典型性结构的发现。作者改用稀疏自编码器（SAE）激活潜在集合的重叠度作为更具可解释性的相似度度量，发现SAE集合在受控玩具模型中可以恢复组合结构，但在人类概念分析中并不比密集嵌入或残差流状态更忠实于人类判断，而是反映模型内部相似性结构。进一步受控语义实验揭示人类概念变化判断与SAE激活集合变化之间存在显著不匹配，表明在理想化设定之外，SAE特征并不能通过简单的词袋特征语义进行组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者质疑Shani等人（2026）使用密集表示的余弦相似度来分析LLM表征与人类概念结构之间关系的方法。他们希望通过使用SAE激活潜在集合的重叠度这一更可解释的相似度度量，来检验LLM表征是否能更忠实地反映人类类别边界和典型性结构。

Method: 作者首先验证了SAE潜在集合级度量的有效性：在受控玩具模型上检验其能否恢复联合式组合结构，并检查其在自然文本中是否诱导语义连贯的邻域。然后，将人类概念分析扩展到SAE集合相似度上，比较SAE激活集合与密集嵌入、残差流状态在恢复人类类别边界和内部典型性方面的表现。最后，在受控语义修改条件下研究激活潜在集合的变化，以探测人类判断与SAE集合变化之间的差距。

Result: 研究发现SAE激活集合并不能比密集嵌入或残差流状态更忠实地恢复人类类别边界或类别内典型性，而是主要跟踪模型内部的相似性结构。受控语义修改实验中，SAE激活集合的变化与人类对概念变化的判断之间存在显著不匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了证据表明，在理想化设定之外，SAE特征不能通过简单的词袋特征语义进行组合。这意味着将SAE特征视为可解释且忠实于人类概念结构的表示可能存在局限，需要更精细的方法来弥合模型表征与人类语义判断之间的差距。

Abstract: Shani et al. (2026) show that LLM representations broadly recover human category boundaries, while failing to reflect fine-grained typicality structure. Their analysis uses cosine similarity over dense model representations. We revisit their approach using overlap over active sparse autoencoder (SAE) latent sets as a more interpretable similarity measure. We first verify that this set-level measure is meaningful: SAE latent sets can recover union-like compositional structure in controlled toy models and induce semantically coherent neighborhoods in natural text. Extending the human-concepts analysis to SAE set similarities, we find that SAE activation sets do not recover human category boundaries or within-category typicality more faithfully than dense embeddings or residual-stream states, but instead track model-internal similarity structure. To probe this gap further, we study active latent sets under well-controlled semantic modifications, revealing a substantial mismatch between human judgements of conceptual change and change in the SAE active set. We interpret this as evidence that, outside idealised settings, SAE features do not compose via simple bag-of-features semantics.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Polyhedral Outer-Approximations for MISOCP: Geometry and Cutting Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10055)
*Yongzheng Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究混合整数二阶锥规划的多面体外逼近（OA）方法，提出了两种改进策略：一是基于切割边际贡献的几何理论分析，开发平衡分离深度与角度新颖性的切割生成策略；二是渐进整性OA框架，从LP松弛逐步过渡到完整MILP。在CBLIB实例和大型AC机组组合模型上的实验表明两种策略具有互补收益，显著降低了外逼近的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数二阶锥规划（MISOCP）通常通过多面体外逼近（OA）求解，该方法通过切割平面迭代强化锥可行域的线性松弛。然而，随着切割数量的增加，新切割的边际贡献迅速下降，导致计算效率低下。本文旨在分析切割贡献的下降规律，并据此设计更高效的切割生成策略和求解框架，以减少外逼近方法的计算负担。

Method: 本文主要包含两部分方法：(1) 几何分析——利用违反度和体积度量，证明新切割的边际贡献随支撑方向接近已有切割方向时至少线性递减，据此提出一种平衡分离深度与角度新颖性的切割生成策略，且该策略具有闭式解；(2) 渐进整性OA框架——从LP松弛出发，经过部分整性松弛（partially integral relaxations）逐步过渡到完整MILP，利用廉价的早期迭代强化松弛后再进行后期的混合整数求解。

Result: 在CBLIB基准实例和大型交流（AC）机组组合模型上的计算实验表明：(1) 所提出的切割策略与渐进整性框架具有互补性收益；(2) 两种方法结合使用时，大幅降低了外逼近方法的计算开销。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了切割边际贡献的线性递减规律，并据此开发了更高效的切割生成策略和渐进整性外逼近框架。实验验证了这两种改进在MISOCP求解中具有互补优势，能够显著提升外逼近方法的计算效率。这项工作为MISOCP的求解提供了新的理论洞见和实用算法改进。

Abstract: Mixed-integer second-order cone programs are commonly solved by polyhedral outer approximation (OA), which iteratively strengthens a linear relaxation of the conic feasible region through cutting planes. We study how such approximations can be constructed more efficiently. First, we analyze the marginal contribution of a newly generated cut relative to cuts already present in the relaxation. Using violation- and volume-based measures, we show that this contribution decreases at least as fast as linearly as the new supporting direction approaches an existing one. Motivated by this geometric analysis, we develop a cut-generation strategy that balances separation depth with angular novelty and admits closed-form constructions. Second, we introduce a progressive-integrality OA framework that proceeds from an LP relaxation through partially integral relaxations before reaching the full MILP, thereby using inexpensive early iterations to strengthen the approximation before later mixed-integer solves. Computational experiments on CBLIB instances and large-scale AC unit-commitment models demonstrate complementary benefits from the proposed cut strategy and progressive integrality, substantially reducing the computational effort of outer approximation.

</details>


### [170] [Thread Scaling of Hexaly on the TDVRPTW across Two Model Encodings. An Experimental Report: External-Function Serialization, Slice-Count Choice, and the Time-Sliced Thread Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10079)
*Florian Rascoussier*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hexaly求解器中带时间窗的时变车辆路径问题（TDVRPTW）的线程扩展性，发现模型编码方式比线程数本身对求解质量影响更大。原生时间片近似编码能有效利用多核并行，而外部回调编码仅使用单个求值工作线程，无法从多线程中获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多线程求解器中，TDVRPTW的线程扩展性高度依赖问题建模方式。现有研究中缺乏对两种编码方式（外部连续函数回调 vs 原生时间片近似）在多线程下的系统对比，需要阐明建模选择如何影响并行效率与求解质量。

Method: 对比两种Python编码：一种通过外部回调评估连续旅行时间函数，另一种将旅行时间函数离散为时间片并由求解器原生评估。通过独立CPU测量和基准面板测试，比较不同线程数（1到16线程）下的求解质量、稳定性和求解过程表现。

Result: 外部函数接口无论请求多少线程，仅使用单一求值工作线程；原生编码则能有效利用分配的所有核心。在基准面板上，更宽的原生搜索在运行结束时及整个搜索过程中都产生更优的解，且显著降低不同随机种子间的方差。近所有配对实验中，线程从1增至16均带来改进。

Conclusion: 编码选择对求解效果的影响大于线程数本身，原生评估带来最大增益，但代价是需要足够精细且独立验证的旅行时间近似。这些结果支持在模型可利用多线程时，多线程是提升解质量和稳定性的实用途径，同时表明建模方法对时变路由中线程扩展性结论至关重要。

Abstract: Thread scaling in Hexaly depends first on how the Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows is modeled. We compare two Python encodings: one evaluates continuous travel-time functions through external callbacks, while the other approximates them with time slices evaluated natively by the solver. Independent CPU measurements show that the supported external-function interface uses a single evaluator worker regardless of the requested thread count. The native encoding, by contrast, makes effective use of the allocated cores. Across our selected benchmark panel, wider native searches produce better solutions both at the end of the run and throughout the search, while substantially reducing variation between seeds. Nearly every paired run improves from one to sixteen threads. The choice of encoding has an even larger impact than the thread count: native evaluation provides the strongest gains, at the cost of a travel-time approximation that must be sufficiently fine and independently checked. These descriptive results support multi-threading as a practical way to improve solution quality and stability when the model can exploit it, and show that the modeling approach is central to thread-scaling conclusions in time-dependent routing.

</details>


### [171] [A lower bound for stepsize-based acceleration of gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10418)
*Jianhao Ma,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑凸优化中仅通过设计步长调度（不加动量等修改）加速梯度下降的问题，提出了一个新的下界 Ω(T^{-1.9319})，证明仅靠步长调度无法将朴素梯度下降加速到最优的 O(T^{-2}) 收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，通过精心设计的步长调度可将朴素梯度下降的收敛速率从教科书式的 O(T^{-1}) 加速到 O(T^{-log_2(1+√2)})，但这类方法的下界研究几乎空白，仅存在针对一般一阶方法的经典 Ω(T^{-2}) 基准。因此需要明确：仅靠步长调度能否达到最优收敛速率？

Method: 作者针对具有预定义非负步长调度的梯度下降，构造了一个新的下界 Ω(T^{-1.9319})，针对最后一次迭代（last-iterate）的收敛速率进行证明。该证明在作者指导下由 GPT-5.6 Sol Pro 开发完成。

Result: 获得了新的下界 Ω(T^{-1.9319})，介于已知的 O(T^{-log_2(1+√2)})（约 T^{-1.2716}）上界与经典最优下界 Ω(T^{-2}) 之间，为步长调度方法的能力边界提供了严格证据。

Conclusion: 步长调度本身无法将朴素梯度下降加速到最优的 O(T^{-2}) 收敛速率，这为步长调度方法的局限性提供了首个严格的理论证据，并暗示若想达到最优速率可能需要引入其他算法层面的修改。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that, for smooth convex optimization, plain gradient descent can be accelerated from its textbook convergence rate of $O(T^{-1})$ (where $T$ denotes the number of iterations) to $O\big(T^{-\log_2(1+\sqrt{2})}\big)$ using carefully designed stepsize schedules alone, without resorting to momentum or other algorithmic modifications. Despite this progress, however, little was known about lower bounds for such methods beyond the classical $Ω(T^{-2})$ benchmark for general first-order methods. In this work, we present a new lower bound of $Ω(T^{-1.9319})$ for the last-iterate convergence rate of gradient descent with predetermined nonnegative stepsize schedules. This result provides rigorous evidence that stepsize schedules alone cannot accelerate plain GD to the optimal $O(T^{-2})$ convergence rate. The proof was developed by GPT-5.6 Sol Pro under the authors' guidance.

</details>


### [172] [A New Approach for Feedback Stabilization and its Application for Data-Driven Control of Polynomial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10158)
*Diego de S. Madeira,Joao Gabriel N. Silva,Wilkley B. Correia,Antonis Papachristodoulou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文受耗散性控制启发，提出了非线性系统状态反馈渐近镇定的新充分条件，并证明该框架适用于基于噪声测量数据的多项式系统数据驱动控制。文中提供了两种基于SOS优化的迭代数据驱动状态反馈设计方法，克服了传统交替(D-K)方法的局限（如需要初始化控制Lyapunov函数），并通过数值算例验证了方法的适用性和优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于SOS的交替(D-K)迭代控制设计方法存在典型局限性，例如需要为控制Lyapunov函数(CLF)提供初始值。同时，基于噪声测量数据的数据驱动多项式系统控制是近年来的研究热点，亟需新的理论框架来提供充分条件并设计高效的控制策略。

Method: 受耗散性控制最新进展的启发，提出非线性系统状态反馈渐近镇定的新充分条件；基于该框架，利用和平方（SOS）优化设计了两种迭代式的数据驱动状态反馈设计算法，适用于含噪测量数据的多项式系统。

Result: 证明了新框架对数据驱动控制（含噪声测量）的适用性；提供了两种基于SOS优化的迭代设计流程；克服了传统交替(D-K)方法中需要初始化CLF的局限；数值算例验证了方法的可行性和优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于耗散性理论的新框架为非线性系统的数据驱动渐近镇定提供了更为灵活且实用的充分条件，两种SOS迭代设计方法在理论和实践上均展现出优越性，为多项式系统的数据驱动控制提供了新工具。

Abstract: Inspired by recent developments in dissipativity-based control, this work proposes new sufficient conditions for asymptotic stabilization of nonlinear systems using state feedback. We prove that this new framework is well suited for data-driven control of polynomial systems using noisy measurements, a topic that has lately attracted considerable attention. Two iterative procedures for data-based state feedback design are provided using the proposed framework, that use sum-of-squares (SOS) optimization. Typical limitations of conventional SOS methods based on alternating (D-K) procedures for controller design, such as the need to provide an initialization for a control-Lyapunov function (CLF), are overcome in this paper. Numerical examples demonstrate the applicability and the advantages of the new strategies.

</details>


### [173] [An Optimal Energy Production Problem with Energy Source Switching and Load Following Nuclear Power Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10197)
*Fabio Baschetti,Alessandro Gnoatto,Athena Picarelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究可再生能源主导系统中，负荷跟踪核电站如何通过最优切换运营模式（增/减/保持输出）来平衡随机剩余需求，在封闭经济（无法售电、缺电需高成本备用）和开放经济（可参与市场交易）两种场景下，利用最优切换问题、Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman拟变分不等式粘性解及单调半Lagrangian数值方案刻画最优策略，揭示缺电成本、切换成本、爬坡能力与市场准入如何塑造核电站的负荷跟踪行为，强调可控低碳容量在可再生密集型系统中的经济价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着天气依赖性可再生能源大量并网，剩余需求的波动性显著增大，充电式低碳灵活性资源的价值随之上升。核电站作为重要的低碳基荷电源，其负荷跟踪能力对系统平衡至关重要，但如何在爬坡限制与运行模式切换成本约束下最优运行，尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 将核电站运营决策建模为有限时域最优切换问题，输出可在增、减、保持三种模式间切换；分别构建封闭经济（超额产量不可售、缺电需昂贵备用）与开放经济（可按市场剩余需求驱动的电价进行交易）两种场景；通过Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman拟变分不等式系统的粘性解刻画价值函数，并采用单调半Lagrangian格式进行数值求解。

Result: 数值结果表明，缺电成本、切换成本、爬坡能力与市场准入共同决定核电站最优负荷跟踪策略；市场准入（开放经济）能显著改变最优运营决策，并且在可再生能源渗透率高的系统中，可控低碳容量具有显著的经济价值。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了负载跟踪核电站作为可控低碳灵活性资源的经济价值，为灵活性补偿机制设计、平衡市场规则制定以及电网互联政策提供了重要启示。

Abstract: The integration of weather-dependent renewable generation increases the volatility of residual demand and raises the value of dispatchable low-carbon flexibility. This paper studies the optimal operation of a load-following nuclear power plant owned by a producer that must balance stochastic residual demand while accounting for ramping limits and costly changes in operating regimes. Nuclear output can be increased, decreased, or kept constant, and the production decision is formulated as a finite-horizon optimal switching problem. We analyze both a closed-economy benchmark, where excess production cannot be sold and shortages require costly back-up generation, and an open-economy setting, where the producer can trade electricity at prices driven by aggregate market residual demand. The value functions are characterized as viscosity solutions of a system of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman quasi-variational inequalities, and optimal policies are computed using a monotone semi-Lagrangian scheme. The numerical results show how shortage costs, switching costs, ramping capability, and market access shape optimal nuclear load following. The analysis highlights the economic value of controllable low-carbon capacity in renewable-intensive systems and provides implications for flexibility remuneration, balancing-market design, and interconnection policy.

</details>


### [174] [Towards Co-Designed Event-Triggered Extremum Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10246)
*Roberto Luo,Pedro Henrique Silva Coutinho,Victor Hugo Pereira Rodrigues,Tiago Roux Oliveira,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有多面体Hessian不确定性非线性映射的事件触发梯度多变量极值搜索方法，提出了一种控制器与触发机制协同设计框架，通过线性矩阵不等式约束的凸优化问题同时设计对角和全矩阵控制器增益，在保证指数收敛的同时最大化可容许触发阈值以减少通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件触发极值搜索方法通常先固定控制器（一般为对角形式），再设计触发机制，这种分离式设计限制了系统性能。此外，已有的方法未充分考虑Hessian不确定性对系统稳定性和收敛性能的影响，且未充分挖掘Hessian耦合信息以提升通信效率。

Method: 提出控制器与触发机制的协同设计（co-design）框架，允许对角和全矩阵控制器增益；将协同设计问题转化为带线性矩阵不等式（LMI）约束的凸优化问题；采用李雅普诺夫分析和平均化分析建立事件触发系统指数稳定性；通过证明Zeno-free性质保证系统可实现性；以最大化可容许触发阈值同时保证预设衰减率为优化目标。

Result: 数值结果表明，全矩阵控制器增益能够实现比对角增益更高的触发阈值和更优的衰减率，说明利用Hessian耦合信息可以显著改善通信效率和收敛性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文证明了控制器与触发机制协同设计的可行性，并展示了全矩阵增益在对角增益无法企及的性能空间中的优势，为多变量极值搜索系统在通信受限场景下的设计提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper studies event-triggered gradient-based multivariable extremum seeking for nonlinear maps with polytopic Hessian uncertainty. Unlike existing event-triggered extremum-seeking methods, which first fix the controller (typically diagonal) and then design the triggering mechanism, the proposed approach jointly synthesizes the controller and the triggering mechanism through a co-design framework that admits both diagonal and full controller gain matrices. The co-design problem is formulated as a convex optimization problem with linear matrix inequality constraints. Its solution guarantees exponential convergence of the average closed-loop system with a prescribed decay rate while maximizing the admissible triggering threshold to reduce communication. Lyapunov and averaging analyses establish exponential stability of the event-triggered system, and Zeno-freeness is proved to guarantee implementability. Numerical results illustrate that diagonal gains cannot achieve the same triggering thresholds and decay rates as the full controller gain matrices, highlighting the benefits of exploiting Hessian coupling information.

</details>


### [175] [Continuous-Time Covariance Steering with Common Free-Final Time: Finite-Horizon Solutions and Infinite-Horizon Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10267)
*Akan Selim,Fengjiao Liu,Siddhartha Ganguly,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间随机线性系统中最优公共自由末态时间问题，旨在将状态协方差从给定的初始协方差矩阵驱动至终端协方差矩阵。建立了SDE约束自由末态时间随机最优控制问题的确定性重构，推导最优性必要条件与有限末态时间存在的充分条件，刻画无穷时域极限行为及哈密顿量灵敏度，并据此开发了信赖域线搜索算法及无穷时域情形检测方法，在三个不同问题上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差控制（covariance steering）是随机最优控制中的重要问题，旨在将系统状态的统计特性（如协方差）从初始分布驱动到期望的终端分布。当最终时间本身是自由决策变量时，问题变得更加复杂。现有研究多聚焦于固定末态时间的协方差控制，而对自由公共末态时间的最优性及其数值求解研究不足。

Method: 首先将SDE约束的自由末态时间随机最优控制问题转化为确定性最优控制问题（即确定性的状态协方差动态系统约束）。随后推导最优性必要条件（基于变分法/庞特里亚金原理）以及末态时间有限的充分条件。接着分析当末态时间趋于无穷时有限时域最优解的渐近行为，刻画该极限状态下的不变集与轨迹，并推导哈密顿量对公共末态时间的灵敏度。基于这些灵敏度分析，设计了信赖域线搜索（trust-region line-search）算法，并提出了无穷时域情形的检测方法。

Result: 所提出的算法在三个问题上得到验证：(a) 说明性的协方差驱动示例；(b) 利用协方差控制进行航天器机动演示；(c) 高斯混合到高斯混合的驱动问题。结果显示算法能够有效求解自由末态时间的协方差控制问题，并能正确检测无穷时域情形。

Conclusion: 该研究为自由末态时间的协方差控制问题提供了完整的理论框架（包括最优性条件、渐近分析和灵敏度推导）与高效数值算法，拓展了协方差控制在更广泛实际场景（如航天器机动、高斯混合分布驱动）中的应用能力。

Abstract: This article studies the optimal common free-final time problem for steering the state covariance of a continuous-time stochastic linear system between prescribed initial and terminal covariance matrices. We first establish a deterministic reformulation of the SDE-constrained free-final time stochastic optimal control problem (SOCP). For the ensuing SOCP, we provide necessary conditions for optimality and establish sufficient conditions for the optimal common final time to be finite. Subsequently, we characterize the asymptotic behavior of the finite-horizon optimal solutions as the final time tends to infinity, the invariant sets and trajectories associated with this limiting regime, and derive the sensitivity of the Hamiltonian with respect to the common final time. Finally, leveraging these sensitivities, we develop a trust-region line-search algorithm together with an infinite-horizon case detection method, and demonstrate its performance on three different problems: (a) an illustrative covariance-steering example, (b) a demonstration of spacecraft maneuver using covariance control, and (c) Gaussian mixture to Gaussian mixture steering.

</details>


### [176] [Kullback-Leibler Mirror-Prox for Measure-Valued Variational Inequalities and Mean-Field Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10293)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ibrahim Ekren,Lu Vy,Ziqing Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究紧状态空间上静态平均场均衡的高效计算问题，将均衡条件表述为概率测度空间上的变分不等式，并提出Kullback-Leibler Mirror-Prox算法——一种熵正则化的Korpelevich外梯度方法，以相对熵近端步替代欧几里得投影。在Lasry-Lions单调性和连续性假设下证明了网格细化遍历平均的收敛性，并获得联合量化迭代与离散误差的有限步Minty残差和近似均衡界；在强单调条件下推导出最后、最优和平均迭代的度量收敛率。此外，发展了KL型Tikhonov正则化以选取相对于参考测度最小化相对熵的均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态平均场均衡（MFE）在经济学、博弈论和粒子系统中有广泛应用，但现有计算方法通常要求成本算子具有可微性、凸性或特定结构，且缺乏针对概率测度空间的直接高效算法。本文旨在为一般（包括非势、不可微）成本算子下的MFE提供可证明收敛的数值算法，同时量化迭代误差与离散化误差。

Method: 将均衡条件转化为概率测度空间上的变分不等式（VI），提出KL Mirror-Prox方法：将Korpelevich外梯度算法中的欧几里得投影替换为相对熵近端步，每个半步包含对当前测度的显式指数重加权，在有限状态空间离散化上实现。采用网格细化技术处理概率测度约束，并结合KL型Tikhonov正则化选择具有最小相对熵的特定均衡。

Result: 在Lasry-Lions单调性和连续性假设下，证明了网格细化遍历平均的收敛性，得到有限迭代的Minty残差界和近似均衡界（联合量化迭代与离散化误差）；在强单调性下，推导出最后、最优及平均迭代的度量收敛率。方法适用于势与非势成本算子，且不需要成本在个体状态上的可微性或凸性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了静态平均场均衡计算的一般性理论框架与算法，所提出的KL Mirror-Prox方法在概率测度空间上具有可证明的收敛保证，突破了传统方法对成本结构的要求，为大规模平均场均衡问题的数值求解提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: We study the computation of static mean-field equilibria on a compact state space by formulating the equilibrium condition as a variational inequality over probability measures. We propose an entropic variant of Korpelevich's extragradient algorithm---the Kullback--Leibler Mirror-Prox method---in which Euclidean projections are replaced by relative-entropy proximal steps. Each half-step is therefore an explicit exponential reweighting of the current measure, implemented on a finite state-space discretization. Under Lasry--Lions monotonicity and continuity assumptions, we prove convergence of mesh-refined ergodic averages and obtain finite-iteration Minty-residual and approximate-equilibrium bounds that jointly quantify iteration and discretization errors. Under strong monotonicity, we derive metric convergence rates for the last, best, and averaged iterates. We also develop a KL-type Tikhonov regularization that selects the equilibrium minimizing relative entropy with respect to a reference measure. The framework applies to potential and nonpotential cost operators and does not require differentiability or convexity of the cost in the individual state.

</details>


### [177] [Attainment Boundaries and Escape Rates in Asymptotically Conic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10341)
*Vinh Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究无界凸集上线性优化的到达边界（attainment boundary）问题，通过凸共轭将回收锥余正性、下有界性与可达性分离为三个嵌套的子集，建立了内扰动的面渐近选择定理，并针对径向渐近锥形epigraph给出了完整的边界三分法与普适标度律，为二阶锥规划导出了显式且尖锐的逃逸、条件数与正则化速率公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无界凸集上的线性优化可能具有有限但不可达的最优值（attainment boundary），此时目标沿某些方向渐进逼近下确界而非达到。经典理论（如下水平集紧致性、Slater条件）不直接刻画此类退化情形，需要系统的边界理论来揭示逃逸方向、最优值渐近行为以及扰动下的正则化机制。

Method: 利用凸共轭将可达性问题转化为共轭空间中三个嵌套子集（回收锥copositivity集、下有界性集、可达性集）的刻画；建立面状（facewise）渐近选择定理，分析内扰动极小元的逃逸方向、blow-up标度及最优值主渐近；对径向渐近锥形epigraph推导边界三分法（有界且达到/有界不达到/无界）与普适标度律（目标倾斜、硬截断、幂正则化）；对平移椭球二阶锥规划给出显式primal-dual公式及逃逸、条件数和正则化率，并证明其具有精确鲁棒优化表示。

Result: 证明了到达边界的三个结构定理：面渐近选择定理刻画了多维临界集下内扰动的逃逸方向与精确标度；径向渐近锥情形下给出完整的三分法和普适标度律；椭球二阶锥规划获得显式且尖锐的逃逸速率、条件数增长和正则化率，并建立了精确鲁棒优化表示。关键发现：在到达边界处，严格原始可行性、零对偶间隙与对偶可达性可以同时成立，而原始可达性却失败。

Conclusion: 本文为无界凸线性优化的退化边界情形提供了统一的几何框架与定量工具，揭示了原始-对偶可达性在边界处的非对称性（原始不可达与对偶可达可并存），并为实际算法在正则化/扰动下的行为提供理论指导。

Abstract: We study attainment boundaries for linear optimization over unbounded convex sets. For epigraphs of finite convex functions, convex conjugacy separates recession-cone copositivity, boundedness below, and attainment through three nested subsets of conjugate space. At a finite but unattained boundary value, we establish a facewise asymptotic selection theorem for inward perturbations of the objective. The theorem identifies the escaping directions of the perturbed minimizers, their precise blow-up scale, and the leading asymptotics of the optimal value; the critical set may be multidimensional, and no radial symmetry is assumed. For radial asymptotically conic epigraphs, we obtain a complete boundary trichotomy and universal scaling laws for objective tilting, hard truncation, and power regularization. For shifted ellipsoidal second-order cone programs, we derive explicit primal--dual formulas together with sharp escape, conditioning, and regularization rates. These models also admit an exact robust-optimization representation. At the attainment boundary, strict primal feasibility, zero duality gap, and dual attainment can coexist with failure of primal attainment.

</details>


### [178] [Submillisecond Sequential Convex Optimization for Powered Landing via Dynamics Condensation and xPIPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10395)
*Wenbo Li,Ziqi Xu,Dai Shen,Shengping Gong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低延迟的压缩顺序凸逼近算法，用于变质量、自由最终时间、二次气动阻力下的动力着陆问题。通过精确块消元将31节点模型缩减为100个原始变量，结合外推比例-积分投影梯度（xPIPG）内层求解器和单精度C实现，在Intel Core i7-10875H上实现374微秒的中位端到端求解时间（P99为512微秒），首次达到亚毫秒级单轨迹顺序凸求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力着陆的在线求解成本主要来自长动力学等式链和内层迭代，现有方法难以满足实时性要求。需要一种低延迟的压缩顺序凸逼近方法，以实现在资源受限的嵌入式平台上快速求解。

Method: 采用精确块消元移除217个中间状态分量和210个区间方程，将模型压缩为100个原始变量和6个终端等式约束；引入低权重能量项和固定二次近端正则化使代理函数强凸且具有可预测曲率；内层使用外推比例-积分投影梯度（xPIPG）求解器，采用固定大小数组、3×3区间求解和融合单遍节点映射实现；使用单精度C语言实现。
