Result: 在Intel Core i7-10875H上，4次外更新和336次xPIPG更新完成一次规划，中位端到端求解时间为374微秒，P99为512微秒；100种初始状态扰动全部通过验证；15-51节点范围的中位时间低于0.7毫秒；独立的高精度一阶保持积分验证给出终端位置误差0.183米。

Conclusion: 在所述模型、硬件、停止准则和时间边界内，这是已知的首个亚毫秒端到端顺序凸求解器，用于单次动力着陆轨迹规划。单遍映射是有意的低成本近似而非精确联合近端算子，因此通过非线性轨迹残差和独立物理检查而非精确KKT收敛性声明来评估代码。

Abstract: Powered landing with variable mass, free final time, and quadratic aerodynamic drag requires the repeated solution of local convex subproblems, whose main online cost lies in the long dynamics-equality chain and the inner iterations. This paper develops a condensed sequential convex approximation designed for low latency. Exact block elimination removes 217 intermediate-state components and 210 interval equations from a 31-node model, leaving 100 primal variables coupled by six terminal equalities. A low-weight energy term and fixed quadratic proximal regularization make the ideal surrogate strongly convex with predictable curvature. The inner solver is an extrapolated proportional--integral projected gradient (xPIPG) implemented with fixed-size arrays, $3\times3$ interval solves, and a fused one-pass node map. The one-pass map is a deliberate low-cost approximation, not the exact joint proximal operator. We therefore evaluate the timed code by nonlinear trajectory residuals and independent physical checks rather than by a claim of exact KKT convergence. The single-precision C implementation completes one plan in four outer updates and 336 xPIPG updates. On an Intel Core i7-10875H, the median end-to-end solve time is \SI{374}{\micro\second} and the P99 value is \SI{512}{\micro\second}. All 100 common initial-state perturbations pass validation, and the median remains below \SI{0.7}{\milli\second} for 15--51 nodes. An independent high-accuracy first-order-hold integration gives a terminal position error of \SI{0.183}{\meter}. Within the stated model, hardware, stopping rule, and timing boundary, this is, to the authors' knowledge, the first submillisecond end-to-end sequential-convex solve for a single powered-landing trajectory.

</details>


### [179] [Indefinite Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Optimal Control Problem for a Markov Regime-Switching Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10597)
*Na Li,Yilin Wei,Harry Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带马尔可夫状态切换参数的不定随机线性二次（SLQ）控制问题，引入松弛补偿器将SLQ控制从正定情形推广到不定情形，分别研究无约束与有约束两种情况下的随机Hamiltonian系统、Riccati方程及最优开环和闭环控制，并通过股票-债券资产配置问题的数值仿真验证了理论框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SLQ控制问题通常要求代价函数中的权重矩阵为正定，但实际问题中常出现不定情形（即权重矩阵非正定），且参数随马尔可夫状态切换变化。需要一种理论框架来覆盖这类更一般的不定SLQ控制问题。

Method: 基于SLQ问题的适定性，引入松弛补偿器将问题从正定情形扩展到不定情形；分别针对无约束和有约束控制情形分析随机Hamiltonian系统，导出最优开环控制；进一步研究相应的Riccati方程，推导最优控制的闭环反馈形式；最后通过股票-债券资产分配问题进行数值仿真验证。

Result: 成功将SLQ控制问题推广至不定情形，为无约束和有约束两种情形均推导出最优开环控制和闭环反馈控制形式，并通过股票-债券资产分配的数值模拟验证了理论框架的有效性及实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的松弛补偿器框架有效解决了含马尔可夫状态切换参数的的不定SLQ控制问题，为复杂随机控制问题提供了新的理论工具，在实际资产配置等金融应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates an indefinite stochastic linear-quadratic (SLQ) control problem with parameters subject to Markov regime-switching. Based on the well-posedness of the SLQ problem, we introduce a relaxed compensator that extends SLQ control problems from the positive definite case to the indefinite case. We analyze the corresponding stochastic Hamiltonian system for both unconstrained and constrained control cases under the indefinite framework and derive the corresponding optimal open-loop controls. We further investigate the associated Riccati equations for both unconstrained and constrained control cases and derive the closed-loop feedback forms of optimal controls. We illustrate the theoretical results with an equity-bond asset allocation problem under un-constrained and non-negative control constraints. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the theoretical framework and demonstrate its practical value in solving complex stochastic control problems with Markov regime-switching.

</details>


### [180] [Randomization and Performance Improvement for Integer Optimal Control with Total Variation Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10624)
*Robert Baraldi,Paul Manns,Lars Mösezahl,Marvin Severitt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文数值分析了两种现有的混合整数PDE约束优化算法（SLIP和Patch-SLIP），并提出了一种新的随机化算法Randomized-Patch-SLIP，该算法在随机选取的域子区域内求解信赖域子问题。文章通过图像去噪和隐身（cloaking）等基准问题对比了三种算法与多种改进技术的组合性能，并发布了配套代码包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数PDE约束优化问题因整数规划的离散性和PDE模型评估的高成本而极具计算挑战性。现有算法虽已有诸多改进，但往往受限于问题规模，缺乏对算法与改进技术最佳组合的系统性数值研究。

Method: 对SLIP和Patch-SLIP两种现有算法进行数值分析，提出并证明了Randomized-Patch-SLIP算法的收敛性——该算法在随机选取的域子区域（patch）上求解信赖域子问题。通过图像去噪和隐身（cloaking）两个基准问题，结合文献中的多种改进技术，系统比较了三种算法的性能组合。

Result: 报告了不同算法与改进技术组合的计算结果，文档化了这些改进与多种求解器配合时的最优组合，并发布了可复现实验的代码包。Randomized-Patch-SLIP被证明收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为混合整数PDE约束优化提供了系统的算法-改进技术性能基准，提出的Randomized-Patch-SLIP算法具备理论收敛保证，同时建立了图像去噪和隐身问题作为标准测试案例，有助于该领域后续算法研究。

Abstract: Mixed-integer PDE-constrained optimization problems are computationally challenging due to both the combinatorial nature of integer programming as well as evaluation of the model. Many algorithms and subsequent performance improvements have been developed to solve these problems, but they are often limited by problem size. We numerically analyze two such algorithms: SLIP and Patch-SLIP, which solve trust-region subproblems over either the full or partial domain, respectively. We additionally propose and prove convergence of a randomized third algorithm, Randomized-Patch-SLIP, which solves trust-region subproblems over randomly selected patches of the domain. We compare performance of all three algorithms with various improvement techniques found throughout the literature; the purpose of this work is to document the best combinations of these improvements in conjunction with various solvers. We additionally establish benchmark problems in image denoising and cloaking. Computational results are reported on combinations of algorithms and improvements, and code used in the experiments is provided as a package.

</details>


### [181] [Robust Unit Commitment in District Heating Networks: Chance-Constrained and CVaR Optimization Under Demand Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10899)
*Annika Buchholz,Janina Zittel,Thorsten Koch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对区域供热网络中热量需求的不确定性问题，将机会约束规划（CC）和条件风险价值（CVaR）优化方法适配到混合整数机组组合问题中，并结合贝叶斯模型生成的需求预测分布，在柏林实际网络和合成基准实例上验证了两种方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域供热网络是能源转型的关键组成部分，但其运行规划面临热量需求显著不确定性的挑战，需要在保障系统可靠性的前提下处理这种波动性。

Method: （1）采用贝叶斯模型生成热量需求时间序列，明确量化预测不确定性并输出预测分布；（2）将机会约束规划和条件风险价值优化适配到区域供热网络的混合整数机组组合问题中，其中CC限制需求未满足的概率，CVaR则惩罚分布尾部的严重短缺。

Result: 在柏林区域供热网络真实数据以及不同规模的合成基准实例上对两种方法进行了评估，比较了它们在解质量、风险暴露和计算开销方面的表现。

Conclusion: 两种鲁棒优化方法均能在需求不确定下计算最优机组组合调度方案，但各自在风险控制和计算效率方面存在权衡，具体表现需根据结果进一步分析。

Abstract: While district heating networks are a key component of the energy transition, their operational planning is challenging due to substantial uncertainty in heat demand. We address this volatility without compromising system reliability. By adapting chance-constrained programming (CC) and conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) optimization to the mixed-integer unit-commitment problem for district-heating networks, we can compute optimal unit-commitment schedules under demand uncertainty. We generate heat demand time series using a Bayesian model, which explicitly quantifies forecasting uncertainty and yields predictive distributions as input to the network optimization. To handle the inherent uncertainty in heat demand, we apply both robust optimization approaches: chance-constrained programming limits the probability of unmet demand, while CVaR optimization penalizes severe shortfalls in the tail of the distribution. We evaluate the approaches both on real-world data from the Berlin district heating network and on a benchmark set of synthetic, realistically parameterized instances of varying sizes. The results compare the two uncertainty-handling methods with respect to solution quality, risk exposure, and computational effort.

</details>


### [182] [Robust Patrol in a Dispersed Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10935)
*Edward Mellor,Kevin Glazebrook,Kyle Lin,Rob Shone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究巡逻者在多个地理分散地点间移动以检测随时间到达的攻击者的巡逻问题。巡逻者可在每个地点花费任意时间搜索，并以地点相关的瞬时检测率检测攻击者，目标是最小化攻击者在任何地点、任何时间保持未被检测的期望时间。当旅行时间可忽略时，给出了最优循环策略；当旅行时间不可忽略时，提出了两种基于周长巡逻和边境巡逻常见实践的循环巡逻模式，推导了期望检测时间公式并提供了最优搜索时间参数的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巡逻问题在安全、国防等场景中具有重要应用，而现有研究在处理旅行时间不可忽略的复杂情况时缺乏系统性方法。作者希望克服这一挑战，为实际巡逻实践提供理论指导和可计算的优化算法。

Method: 论文首先分析旅行时间可忽略的特殊情形，证明存在最优循环策略，即巡逻者将固定比例的努力连续分配给每个地点。对于旅行时间不可忽略的一般情形，提出了两类循环巡逻模式（分别基于周长巡逻和边境巡逻的常见实践），推导了两类模式下的期望检测时间解析公式，并设计了寻找最优搜索时间参数的算法。

Result: 通过多个数值例子验证了所提出的两类循环巡逻模式表现良好，同时数值实验表明最优巡逻策略高度依赖于每个巡逻问题的具体结构和参数。

Conclusion: 论文为具有旅行时间的巡逻问题提供了两类实用的循环巡逻模式及其最优参数求解算法，并通过数值实验揭示了最优策略对问题结构和参数的敏感性，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We consider a patrol problem in which a patroller moves among several geographically dispersed locations to detect attackers that arrive over time. Once the patroller arrives at a location, they can spend any amount of time searching for attackers at that location---and detect an attacker there with some location-dependent instantaneous detection rate---before moving to a different location. The objective of the patroller is to minimize the expected time an attacker stays undetected at a location, regardless of where and when the attack occurs. In the special case where travel times are negligible, we elucidate an optimal cyclic policy in which the patroller allocates a fixed fraction of their effort to each location continuously. The patrol problem becomes significantly more challenging when travel times cannot be ignored. We introduce two types of cyclic patrol patterns based on common patrol practice for perimeter patrol and border patrol, respectively, and derive formulae for the expected time to detect an attack in both cases. We also provide an algorithm for finding the best search time parameters in both of these cases. We give several examples where these cycle types perform well and numerically demonstrate that the optimal patrol policy depends highly on the structure and parameters of each patrol problem.

</details>


### [183] [Threshold Structure of Optimal Policies in Restart POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10936)
*Konstantin Avrachenkov,Alexey Piunovskiy,Yi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般Borel状态空间上的Restart POMDP（部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程），控制器可选择让隐藏状态演化或重启系统并观察新状态。通过利用充分统计量表示（最后观察到的状态和重启以来的经过时间），将问题转化为完全可观测的MDP，并在一步代价劣化条件下证明了折扣和总无折扣代价准则下的最优策略阈值结构。在状态空间为偏序且核随机单调时，进一步证明最优阈值关于状态非增。对于平均代价准则，在几何遍历性和瞬态增益支配的额外假设下，通过消失折扣方法建立了类似的阈值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Restart POMDP在维护、医疗决策、机器学习等领域有广泛应用，但其最优策略的结构性性质（如阈值结构）尚未被系统研究。作者旨在揭示在自然条件下最优策略的简单结构，从而为实际决策提供可操作的指导。

Method: 利用最后观察到的状态和重启以来的经过时间作为充分统计量，将POMDP转化为完全可观测的MDP；然后在一步代价劣化（one-step cost deterioration）条件下，分别针对折扣准则和总无折扣准则证明阈值策略的最优性；在状态空间偏序和核随机单调假设下分析阈值的单调性；对平均代价准则，假设几何遍历性和瞬态增益支配，通过消失折扣方法（vanishing discount approach）证明阈值的均匀有界性和相对值函数的性质。

Result: 证明在一步代价劣化条件下，折扣和总无折扣代价准则下最优策略具有关于经过时间的阈值结构；在状态空间偏序和随机单调核条件下，最优阈值关于状态非增；在几何遍历性和瞬态增益支配假设下，通过消失折扣方法建立了平均代价准则下类似的阈值结果，并证明了最优阈值和相对值函数的均匀有界性。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地建立了Restart POMDP最优策略的阈值结构理论，涵盖了折扣、总无折扣和平均三种代价准则，为实际应用提供了严格的理论基础。研究表明，在相对一般的条件下，虽然状态部分可观测，最优重启策略仍可简化为基于当前状态和经过时间的阈值策略。

Abstract: We study a Restart POMDP (Partially Observable Markov Decision Process) on a general Borel state space, where the controller either lets the hidden state evolve unobserved or restarts the system and observes the new state. Exploiting a sufficient-statistic representation consisting of the last observed state and the elapsed time since restart, we reduce the problem to a fully observed MDP. Under a natural one-step cost deterioration condition, we prove that optimal policies have a threshold structure in the elapsed time for both the discounted and total undiscounted cost criteria. When the state space is partially ordered and the kernel is stochastically monotone, we further show that the optimal threshold is nonincreasing in the state. For the average cost criterion, under additional assumptions of geometric ergodicity and domination of the transient gain, we establish analogous threshold results via the vanishing discount approach, after showing the uniform boundedness of the optimal thresholds and relative value functions.

</details>


### [184] [Prescribed-performance position tracking for infinite-dimensional passive systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10940)
*Anthony Hastir,Timo Reis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性无穷维无源系统提出了一种非线性递归漏斗控制器，该控制器结合动态增益监督器，适用于分布控制/观测和边界控制/观测系统。控制器直接利用无源输出作为导数信息而非数值微分，并在附加位置相容严格耗散条件下，证明了增益最终恒定的全局有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机械应用中，位置控制通常基于测量的无源速度输出，但现有的漏斗控制方法主要处理无源输出直接作为受控输出的情况。当受控输出为无源输出的积分时（即位置控制），需要新的控制设计。此外，需要避免数值微分带来噪声放大问题，并确保全局稳定性和递推误差的性能保证。

Method: 在系统节点框架下，提出非线性递归漏斗控制器与动态增益监督器。监督器在归一化递归误差接近预设监控水平时自动增加阻尼增益。控制器仅使用跟踪误差及其导数（导数直接从无源输出获得），通过递归设计实现漏斗性能约束。

Result: 证明了闭环轨迹的全局存在性和唯一性、规定的漏斗性能以及跟踪误差导数和无源输出的全局有界性。在附加的位置相容严格耗散条件下，监督增益仅有限次增加并最终恒定，从而增益、内部状态和控制输入全局有界，递归误差漏斗裕度对时间一致。

Conclusion: 该控制器为无穷维无源系统的位置控制提供了一种无需数值微分的鲁棒方案，并通过边界驱动的欧拉-伯努利梁（具有均匀正分布阻尼）的例子验证了理论结果的有效性。

Abstract: We study funnel control for linear infinite-dimensional passive systems whose controlled output is obtained by integrating the passive output. In mechanical applications, this corresponds to position control based on a measured passive velocity output. Within the system-node framework, we propose a nonlinear recursive funnel controller with a dynamic gain supervisor for systems with distributed or boundary control and observation. The supervisor automatically increases a damping gain whenever the normalized recursive error approaches a prescribed monitoring level. The controller uses the tracking error and its derivative, with the latter obtained directly from the passive output rather than by numerical differentiation. We prove global existence and uniqueness of the closed-loop trajectory, prescribed funnel performance, and global boundedness of the derivative tracking error and the passive output. Under an additional position-compatible strict dissipativity condition, the supervised gain is increased only finitely many times and eventually becomes constant. Consequently, the gain, the internal state, and the control input are globally bounded, and the recursive-error funnel margin is uniform in time. The results are illustrated by a boundary-actuated Euler-Bernoulli beam with uniformly positive distributed damping.

</details>


### [185] [DD-suite: A cross-platform package to build Decision Diagrams for optimization purposes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10957)
*Antonia F. Blanco,Margarita Castro,Rodrigo Toro Icarte*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DD-suite，一个跨平台、开源的决策图（DD）软件包，支持Python和C++两种语言，用于构建和操作针对离散优化的决策图。该包实现了DD约简、最短路径求解上下界、可视化工具及自动化测试，提供了四种组合问题的示例，并被设计为可扩展的构建模块。实验表明C++实现比Python快5-6倍，且与专用Rust框架ddo性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管决策图已成为离散优化的强大工具，支持割生成过程、分解方法和专用分支定界搜索等多种算法，但其采用率仍然有限。主要原因在于大多数现有的DD代码是针对特定算法或应用定制的，难以复用。

Method: DD-suite通过共享建模接口提供Python和C++两种实现，允许用户以递归形式为任何离散优化问题构建精确、受限和松弛DD。软件包含DD约简过程、用于获得原始和对偶界的最短路径例程、可视化工具以及广泛的自动化测试套件。此外还提供文档、支持网页和四种组合问题的示例，并支持用户添加新的构造机制或在生成的图上运行自定义算法。

Result: 数值实验表明，C++实现比Python实现快5-6倍并生成完全相同的图，且与专用Rust框架ddo相比保持在较小的常数因子内，证实了DD-suite兼具可访问性、可扩展性和竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DD-suite不是封闭的求解器，而是设计为可扩展的构建模块，用户可以在其上构建自定义算法（如文中展示的嵌入先进MIP求解器的DD基割平面方法），实现了易用的代码基础与竞争性性能的结合。

Abstract: Decision diagrams (DDs) have become a powerful tool for discrete optimization, supporting a wide range of algorithms that span cut-generation procedures, decomposition methods, and specialized branch-and-bound searches. Despite this growth, their adoption remains limited, partly because most existing DD code is tailored to a specific algorithm or application and is therefore hard to reuse. We introduce DD-suite, a cross-platform, open-source software package for building and manipulating DDs for discrete optimization. DD-suite is available in both Python and C++ through a shared modeling interface, and lets users construct exact, restricted, and relaxed DDs for any discrete optimization problem expressed in recursive form. The package implements the DD reduction procedure, shortest-path routines for obtaining primal and dual bounds, a visualization tool, and an extensive automated test suite. Furthermore, it includes extensive documentation, a support webpage, and ready-to-use examples for four combinatorial problems. Rather than a closed solver, DD-suite is designed as an extensible building block: users can add new construction mechanisms or run custom algorithms on top of the resulting diagram, as we illustrate with a DD-based cutting plane procedure embedded in a state-of-the-art mixed-integer programming solver. Our numerical experiments show that the C++ implementation is 5-6 times faster than the Python one while producing identical diagrams, and remains within a small constant factor of ddo, a specialized Rust framework, confirming that DD-suite combines an accessible, extensible codebase with competitive performance.

</details>


### [186] [Gromov-Wasserstein Quantization and Clustering: Structure, Rates, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11016)
*Florian Beier,Stephan Eckstein*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）量化问题，即在量化点分布的同时也对空间的环境几何进行聚类，证明了GW量化问题解的存在性，给出了类似k-means（Lloyd算法）的数值近似方法，计算了欧几里得几何下的量化速率，并通过数值实验展示了其在3D形状测地距离和神经网络结构化剪枝等领域的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的k-means等基于质心的聚类方法与Wasserstein距离下的量化问题紧密相关，但Wasserstein量化只能在固定空间内对点进行聚类，无法同时聚类空间本身的环境几何。这限制了其在需要同时考虑几何结构的场景（如3D形状分析、神经网络剪枝）中的应用，因此需要引入Gromov-Wasserstein量化来弥补这一不足。

Method: 本文首先证明GW量化问题解的存在性，并给出一个刻画/表征（characterization），据此提出类比k-means算法（即Lloyd算法）的数值求解方法。随后针对GW上下文中常用的欧几里得几何计算量化速率，并将其与标准的Wasserstein量化速率进行对比。最后通过数值实验验证所提算法的效果。

Result: 证明了GW量化问题解的存在性；提出的类Lloyd算法能够有效近似GW量化问题；推导了欧几里得几何下的GW量化速率并与Wasserstein量化速率建立联系；数值实验表明该方法可应用于3D形状的测地距离聚类和神经网络结构化剪枝，且近似质量通常接近理论最优速率。

Conclusion: GW量化将聚类的范畴从固定空间内的点扩展到空间几何本身，提供了超越传统聚类方法的新建模能力。所提出的数值求解算法在实践中表现良好，量化精度接近理论最优，说明GW量化是一个有前景且实用的聚类与量化框架。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental class of data analysis techniques with the most important representatives being centroid-based methods like $k$-means. Such methods are strongly connected to quantization problems, which aim to approximate general probability measures with discrete ones. For example, $k$-means corresponds to quantization with respect to the Wasserstein distance. While Wasserstein quantization clusters points within a fixed space, this paper studies Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) quantization, which additionally aims at clustering the ambient geometry of the space. We show existence of solutions to the GW quantization problem and give a characterization that justifies an analogue to the $k$-means algorithm (Lloyd's algorithm) to approximate them numerically. We further calculate the quantization rate for usual Euclidean geometries that are used in the GW context, and relate it to standard Wasserstein quantization rates. Finally, numerical experiments show that GW quantization opens up many modeling possibilities beyond normal clustering methods (e.g., for geodesic distances of 3D shapes or structured pruning of neural networks) and that the introduced algorithm leads to useful numerical solutions with approximation quality often in line with theoretically optimal rates.

</details>


### [187] [Integrated Surgical Scheduling with Weekend Bed Occupancy Management: A Column Generation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11018)
*Sara Cambiaghi,Saverio Basso,Davide Duma,Matteo Salani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个联合优化框架，同时解决主手术排程问题（Master Surgical Scheduling Problem）和手术病例分配问题（Surgical Case Assignment Problem），在周末床位容量约束下，通过多目标混合整数线性规划（MILP）和列生成（Column Generation）算法，优化手术室利用率、患者优先级权重及周末床位占用，并支持三种排程政策，实验证明CG方法在大规模实例上优于直接MILP求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院在资源有限的情况下需要同时协调手术室的排程和患者病例的分配，而现有研究通常将主手术排程问题和手术病例分配问题分开处理，忽略了它们在床位容量尤其是周末床位减少时的相互影响，导致整体运营效率不佳。

Method: 将问题建模为多准则MILP，同时决定专科和外科医生分配到手术室时段、患者选择及分配、手术排序，并考虑周末床位减少。采用列生成算法，价格子问题被构造为资源受限最短路径问题（RCSPP），支持块式、开放式和改良块式三种排程政策。

Result: 计算实验表明，列生成方法在解质量和计算时间上均优于直接求解MILP，尤其是在大规模实例上优势明显。同时分析并揭示了周末床位可用性、手术室利用率和患者优先级总量之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合优化框架能够有效帮助医院在有限资源下平衡患者可及性、运营效率和员工工作量，为医院管理者提供了有价值的决策支持和见解。

Abstract: This paper addresses the integrated Master Surgical Scheduling Problem and Surgical Case Assignment Problem under weekend bed capacity constraints. We propose a joint optimization framework that simultaneously determines the assignment of specialties and surgeons to Operating Room (OR) blocks, the selection and assignment of patients to these blocks, and the sequencing of surgeries, while accounting for reduced bed availability during weekends. The model is formulated as a multi-criteria Mixed-Integer Linear Program (MILP) that prioritizes the total priority-weighted amount of scheduled patients, minimizes excess weekend bed occupancy, and maximizes OR utilization. To solve large instances, we develop a Column Generation (CG) algorithm in which the pricing subproblem is formulated as a Resource-Constrained Shortest Path Problem. The framework accommodates block, open, and modified block scheduling policies. Computational experiments show that the CG approach outperforms the direct MILP solution in terms of solution quality and computational time, particularly for larger instances. We further analyze the trade-offs between weekend bed availability, OR utilization, and total patient priority. Our results provide managerial insights into how hospitals can balance patient access, operational efficiency, and staff workload when resources are limited.

</details>


### [188] [Certificate-based Synthesis of Coordinated Droop Control for Heterogeneous Radial Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11141)
*Georgios Pantazis,Michelle Chong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对下垂控制的低压馈线电压认证问题，提出了一种基于虚拟协调与仿射前馈补偿的控制器设计方法，将联合控制器与电压证书设计转化为线性规划，在异质网络中实现更紧致、空间信息更丰富的电压包络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的下垂控制低压馈线电压证书基于全局最坏情况量构建，在异质网络中会掩盖电压风险的来源位置，并随着馈线灵敏度累积在下游变得越来越保守。现有方法仅能评估给定控制器，无法指导控制器设计，导致证书保守度难以根本改善。

Method: 1) 推导确定性、全时段、母线级电压包络，保留局部扰动界、下垂斜率、逆变器限幅和馈线相关灵敏度，并将最坏情况证书作为特例；2) 提出带虚拟协调和仿射前馈补偿的下垂控制架构，重塑有效电压灵敏度以缓解网络拓扑导致的界劣化；3) 通过缩放变换将联合控制器与证书设计转化为线性规划，保证前向不变性和无功功率储备约束满足；4) 控制器仅利用选定的通信链路。

Result: 在五用户住宅馈线和含四个馈线的26用户农村网络两个benchmark上验证，所得证书比传统最坏情况证书更紧致且更具空间信息性，同时满足逆变器限幅约束。

Conclusion: 电压证书不仅可以用于评估控制器，还可以主动用于设计控制器。所提出的虚拟协调与仿射前馈架构可在异质低压馈线中产生更紧、空间可解释的电压包络，且保证所有容许扰动下的电压与逆变器约束，仅需少量通信链路即可实现。

Abstract: Voltage certificates for droop-controlled low voltage feeders are often constructed from global worst-case quantities. In heterogeneous feeders, such bounds can hide where voltage risk arises and become increasingly conservative downstream as feeder sensitivities accumulate. This paper shows that voltage certificates can be used not only to assess a given controller, but also to design it. Specifically, we derive deterministic all-time, bus-wise voltage envelopes that retain local disturbance bounds, droop slopes, inverter limits, and feeder dependent sensitivities, while recovering the worst-case certificate as a special case. To overcome the deterioration of these bounds induced by the network topology, we introduce a droop architecture with virtual coordination and affine feedforward compensation that reshapes the effective voltage sensitivity. A scaling transformation converts the joint controller and certificate design into a linear program which ensures forward invariance and the satisfaction of reactive power reserve constraints. The controller uses only selected communication links and guarantees the certified voltage and inverter bounds for all admissible disturbances. Our certificates and methodology are evaluated on two radial low voltage network benchmarks: a five-customer residential feeder and a 26-customer rural network comprising four feeders. The studies demonstrate tighter and more spatially informative certificates, while respecting inverter limits.

</details>
